User Story 28956: korespondence - tiskové výstupy

tutorial na korespondenci je tady

"C:\Users\karel.skrabal\Documents\Test1\Source\korespondence-module-notes.md"


-----------------------x

# ===== KORESPONDENCE EXCEL EXPORT — CURL TEST SCRIPT =====
  # Database: Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33
  # StavbaId: 1268
  # Run in Git Bash

  # Step 1 — Login (get pre-auth token)
  PREAUTH=$(curl -sk -X POST https://localhost:44301/api/anonym/auth/authenticate/master \
    -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
    -d '{"userName":"skrabal@proconom.cz","password":"YOUR_PASSWORD","idp":1}' | jq -r '.authToken')

  echo "PREAUTH=$PREAUTH"

  # Step 2 — Database token
  TOKEN=$(curl -sk -X POST https://localhost:44301/api/auth/databasetoken \
    -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
    -H "Authorization: Bearer $PREAUTH" \
    -d '{"database":"Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33"}' | jq -r '.uzivatel.token')

  echo "TOKEN=$TOKEN"

  # Step 3 — Excel export (stavbaId = 1268)
  curl -sk "https://localhost:44301/api/Correspondence/constructions/1268/excel" \
    -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" \
    -o "/c/Users/karel.skrabal/Documents/Test1/Source/Korespondence_export.xlsx" \
    -w "\nHTTP_CODE: %{http_code}\nSIZE: %{size_download}\n"

  Notes:
  - Replace YOUR_PASSWORD with your actual password
  - Replace Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33 with any other DB name as needed
  - Replace 1268 with any other construction xID


--------------------x

What it does

  Adds a new API endpoint that exports the entire correspondence of a construction project to an Excel file (.xlsx).

  Endpoint:
  GET /correspondence/constructions/{ConstructionId}/excel

  The Excel has 2 sheets:

  Sheet 1: "Databáze korespondence" (Letter Database)

  Landscape, fit-to-page. Blue header row. Columns:

  ┌─────┬──────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │ Col │      Header      │                                   Source                                    │
  ├─────┼──────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ A   │ Odesílatel       │ Sender name (internal user or external contact)                             │
  ├─────┼──────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ B   │ Hlavní Pod-čl.   │ First sub-article from Podclanky (comma-split)                              │
  ├─────┼──────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ C   │ Vedlejší Pod-čl. │ Remaining sub-articles                                                      │
  ├─────┼──────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ D   │ Číslo dopisu     │ Identifikator                                                               │
  ├─────┼──────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ E   │ Číslo Události   │ Linked event identifiers (joined)                                           │
  ├─────┼──────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ F   │ Předmět dopisu   │ Nazev                                                                       │
  ├─────┼──────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ G   │ Datum            │ Send date or upload date (dd.MM.yyyy)                                       │
  ├─────┼──────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ H   │ Odpověď na dopis │ Identifiers of letters this answers to                                      │
  ├─────┼──────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ I   │ Stav             │ State: Koncept/Odesláno/Neodesláno/Ke schválení/Schváleno/Zamítnuto/Smazáno │
  ├─────┼──────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ J   │ Přílohy          │ Attachment file names (semicolon-separated)                                 │
  ├─────┼──────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ K   │ Poznámka         │ Vyjadreni (statement/comment)                                               │
  └─────┴──────────────────┴─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  Filters out deleted letters. Sorted by Identifikator.

  Sheet 2: "Přehled Claimů a Změn" (Claims & Changes Overview)

  Has a category header row ("Všeobecné" | "Předběžný časový a finanční dopad"). 13 columns:

  ┌─────┬───────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │ Col │        Header         │                                 Source                                  │
  ├─────┼───────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ A   │ Typ                   │ Action type: Změna/Claim/Zlepšení/various notifications (FIDIC-related) │
  ├─────┼───────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ B   │ Č. Události           │ Event identifier                                                        │
  ├─────┼───────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ C   │ Podstata Události     │ Event name                                                              │
  ├─────┼───────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ D   │ SO                    │ (empty — not linked in model yet)                                       │
  ├─────┼───────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ E   │ First notice          │ ANO/NE                                                                  │
  ├─────┼───────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ F   │ Č. Claimu/Změny       │ (empty — no field on action)                                            │
  ├─────┼───────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ G   │ Článek FIDIC, SoD     │ Clanky from action                                                      │
  ├─────┼───────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ H   │ Aktuální stav         │ Oznámeno/Dohodnuto/V posouzení                                          │
  ├─────┼───────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ I   │ Časový dopad          │ DopadCas (numeric)                                                      │
  ├─────┼───────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ J   │ Stav dopadu (čas)     │ Same state as H                                                         │
  ├─────┼───────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ K   │ Finanční dopad        │ DopadCena (formatted #,##0.00)                                          │
  ├─────┼───────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ L   │ Stav dopadu (finance) │ Same state as H                                                         │
  ├─────┼───────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ M   │ Poznámky              │ Event + action notes combined with "                                    │
  └─────┴───────────────────────┴─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  Events with no actions get a single row. Events with actions get one row per action.

  Helper methods

  - GetSenderName — resolves sender from either internal user or partner contact
  - SplitPodclanky — splits comma-delimited sub-article string into main + secondary
  - GetLetterStateText — enum → Czech label (exhaustive switch)
  - GetActionTypeText — 12 action types including FIDIC-style notifications
  - GetActionStateText — 4 states

  Tech details

  - Uses Syncfusion XlsIO for Excel generation
  - Returns FileResponsePCM (stream + content type + filename)
  - Filename: Korespondence_{stavba.Znacka}.xlsx
  - Uses AsSplitQuery() to avoid cartesian explosion from multiple includes
  - All queries are AsNoTracking()

● Now I have the full picture. Here's my analysis:

  What I found

  GetHasSubfolders (EFModels\CustomModels\DDokumenty.cs:28-33):
  public bool GetHasSubfolders(DB_DevelopContext ctx)
  {
      return ctx.DVazby.Where(x => x.TabulkaId == XId && x.Tabulka == "d_dokumenty")
          .Include(x => x.KDokumentyNavigation)
          .Any(x => x.KDokumentyNavigation.Slozka);
  }
  Checks if the folder has any child records that are folders (Slozka = true). Exactly what we need.

  GetChildrenOfEntity (DocumentPermissionManager.cs:27-45):
  - Already populates Document = x.KDokumentyNavigation (line 42) — so the HasChildResolver has its source member available.
  - Returns IQueryable<DDokumentyParented>, materialized by _mapper.Map<List<>>() in the handler — so resolver DB queries will work
   fine.

  Proposed Fix

  One line change — wire up the existing HasChildResolver that was built for this purpose but never connected.

  FILE: Test_Nove_API\TestApi\v1\DTOS\Dokument\SlozkaPravaDTO.cs
  CLASS: SlozkaPravaDTOProfile
  METHOD: constructor
  LINE: 30

  // BEFORE
  .ForMember(d => d.HasChildren, o => o.MapFrom(s => true))

  // AFTER
  .ForMember(d => d.HasChildren, o => o.MapFrom<HasChildResolver, DDokumenty>(s => s.Document))

  Performance note: This creates an N+1 pattern (one DVazby.Any() query per folder). But the Group Permissions tree only loads one
  level at a time, and folder counts per level are typically small (single digits to low dozens), so it's acceptable.

  No other files change. The resolver, the Document property, and the GetHasSubfolders method are all already in place — they just
  weren't connected.

  Want me to apply this change?

  1. Root folders under a stavba (top level of tree — what GetChildrenOfEntity returns for TYP_UZLU.STAVBA):
  -- Replace @StavbaId with the actual stavba xID
  DECLARE @StavbaId INT = ???;

  SELECT d.xID, d.Nazev, d.Pojmenovani, d.Slozka,
         (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_vazby v2
              INNER JOIN [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_dokumenty d2
                  ON v2.k_dokumenty = d2.xID
          WHERE v2.tabulka_id = d.xID AND v2.tabulka = 'd_dokumenty'
                AND d2.Slozka = 1 AND d2.Smazano IS NULL) AS SubfolderCount
  FROM [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_vazby v
  INNER JOIN [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_dokumenty d
      ON v.k_dokumenty = d.xID
  WHERE v.tabulka_id = @StavbaId
    AND v.tabulka = 's_stavby'
    AND d.Slozka = 1
    AND d.Smazano IS NULL
  ORDER BY d.Nazev;

                AND d2.Slozka = 1 AND d2.Smazano IS NULL) AS SubfolderCount

  INNER JOIN [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_dokumenty d
      ON v.k_dokumenty = d.xID
  WHERE v.tabulka_id = @FolderId
    AND v.tabulka = 'd_dokumenty'
    AND d.Slozka = 1
    AND d.Smazano IS NULL
  ORDER BY d.Nazev;

  3. Verify folder 9520 specifically (the bug case — should show 0 subfolders):
  -- All children of folder 9520 (files + folders)
  SELECT d.xID, d.Nazev, d.Slozka,

  FROM [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_vazby v

    AND d.Smazano IS NULL

  4. What GetHasSubfolders checks (the exact logic the resolver runs):
  -- Returns 1 if folder has subfolders, 0 if not (matches GetHasSubfolders)
  SELECT CASE WHEN EXISTS (
      SELECT 1 FROM [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_vazby v
      INNER JOIN [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_dokumenty d
          ON v.k_dokumenty = d.xID
      WHERE v.tabulka_id = 9520 AND v.tabulka = 'd_dokumenty' AND d.Slozka = 1
  ) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS HasSubfolders;

  Note: The join goes d_dokumenty → d_vazby → s_stavby. If a folder is nested deeper (not directly linked to stavba via d_vazby),
  it may not show up. In that case, we'd need to walk up the d_vazby chain recursively. Let me know if any rows are missing.

❯ it shows nothing

● The folder is nested deep — it's not directly linked to s_stavby via d_vazby. The link goes through parent folders. Let's walk up
   the chain from folder 9520:

  -- Walk up the d_vazby chain from folder 9520 to find the stavba
  ;WITH chain AS (
      SELECT v.k_dokumenty, v.tabulka_id, v.tabulka, 0 AS depth
      FROM [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_vazby v
      WHERE v.k_dokumenty = 9520 AND v.tabulka = 'd_dokumenty'

      UNION ALL

      SELECT v.k_dokumenty, v.tabulka_id, v.tabulka, c.depth + 1
      FROM [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_vazby v
      INNER JOIN chain c ON v.k_dokumenty = c.tabulka_id AND c.tabulka = 'd_dokumenty'
      WHERE v.tabulka IN ('d_dokumenty', 's_stavby')
  )
  SELECT c.*,
         d.Nazev AS DokumentNazev, d.folder_path,
         s.xID AS StavbaId, s.nazev AS StavbaNazev, s.znacka AS StavbaZnacka
  FROM chain c
  LEFT JOIN [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_dokumenty d ON d.xID = c.tabulka_id AND c.tabulka = 'd_dokumenty'
  LEFT JOIN [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.s_stavby s ON s.xID = c.tabulka_id AND c.tabulka = 's_stavby'
  ORDER BY c.depth;

  This recursively follows the d_vazby chain upward until it hits s_stavby. Should show each parent folder along the way and the
  stavba at the top.

● Now we have the full chain. The stavba is xID=21, znacka DS202401906. Now let's get the original query working — join through the
   chain to get the stavba info for all folders under that path:

  SELECT d.xID, d.Nazev, d.Pojmenovani, d.Slozka, d.folder_path, d.Smazano,
         21 AS StavbaId,
         'REKONSTRUKCE A STAVEBNÍ ÚPRAVY MĚSTSKÉHO PLAVECKÉHO BAZÉNU V LIBERCI' AS StavbaNazev,
         'DS202401906' AS StavbaZnacka,

                AND d2.Slozka = 1 AND d2.Smazano IS NULL) AS SubfolderCount
  FROM [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_dokumenty d

  2. Full folder tree under stavba 21 (the chain we found):
  ;WITH FolderTree AS (
      -- Level 0: root folders directly under stavba 21
      SELECT d.xID, d.Nazev, d.Pojmenovani, d.Slozka, d.folder_path, d.Smazano,
             0 AS depth
      FROM [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_vazby v
      INNER JOIN [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_dokumenty d
          ON v.k_dokumenty = d.xID
      WHERE v.tabulka_id = 21 AND v.tabulka = 's_stavby'
        AND d.Slozka = 1 AND d.Smazano IS NULL

      UNION ALL

      -- Recurse into subfolders
      SELECT d.xID, d.Nazev, d.Pojmenovani, d.Slozka, d.folder_path, d.Smazano,
             ft.depth + 1
      FROM FolderTree ft
      INNER JOIN [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_vazby v
          ON v.tabulka_id = ft.xID AND v.tabulka = 'd_dokumenty'
      INNER JOIN [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_dokumenty d
          ON v.k_dokumenty = d.xID
      WHERE d.Slozka = 1 AND d.Smazano IS NULL
  )
  SELECT xID, REPLICATE('  ', depth) + Nazev AS TreeView, folder_path, depth,
         (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_vazby v2
              INNER JOIN [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_dokumenty d2
                  ON v2.k_dokumenty = d2.xID
          WHERE v2.tabulka_id = FolderTree.xID AND v2.tabulka = 'd_dokumenty'
                AND d2.Slozka = 1 AND d2.Smazano IS NULL) AS SubfolderCount,
         (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_vazby v3
              INNER JOIN [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_dokumenty d3
                  ON v3.k_dokumenty = d3.xID
          WHERE v3.tabulka_id = FolderTree.xID AND v3.tabulka = 'd_dokumenty'
                AND d3.Slozka = 0 AND d3.Smazano IS NULL) AS FileCount
  FROM FolderTree
  ORDER BY folder_path, Nazev;

  3. Everything inside folder 9520 (2024_11_15(25M)) — files and subfolders:
  SELECT d.xID, d.Nazev, d.Pojmenovani, d.Slozka, d.folder_path, d.Smazano,
         CASE WHEN d.Slozka = 1 THEN 'FOLDER' ELSE 'FILE' END AS Type
  FROM [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_vazby v

      ON v.k_dokumenty = d.xID
  WHERE v.tabulka_id = 9520 AND v.tabulka = 'd_dokumenty'
    AND d.Smazano IS NULL
  ORDER BY d.Slozka DESC, d.Nazev;

  1. Full folder tree under stavba 28:
  ;WITH FolderTree AS (
      SELECT d.xID, d.Nazev, d.Pojmenovani, d.Slozka, d.folder_path, d.Smazano,
             0 AS depth
      FROM [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_vazby v

        AND d.Slozka = 1 AND d.Smazano IS NULL

      UNION ALL

      SELECT d.xID, d.Nazev, d.Pojmenovani, d.Slozka, d.folder_path, d.Smazano,
             ft.depth + 1
      FROM FolderTree ft
      INNER JOIN [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_vazby v
          ON v.tabulka_id = ft.xID AND v.tabulka = 'd_dokumenty'
      INNER JOIN [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_dokumenty d
          ON v.k_dokumenty = d.xID
      WHERE d.Slozka = 1 AND d.Smazano IS NULL
  )
  SELECT xID, REPLICATE('  ', depth) + Nazev AS TreeView, folder_path, depth,
         (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_vazby v2
              INNER JOIN [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_dokumenty d2
                  ON v2.k_dokumenty = d2.xID
          WHERE v2.tabulka_id = FolderTree.xID AND v2.tabulka = 'd_dokumenty'
                AND d2.Slozka = 1 AND d2.Smazano IS NULL) AS SubfolderCount,
         (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_vazby v3
              INNER JOIN [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_dokumenty d3
                  ON v3.k_dokumenty = d3.xID
          WHERE v3.tabulka_id = FolderTree.xID AND v3.tabulka = 'd_dokumenty'
                AND d3.Slozka = 0 AND d3.Smazano IS NULL) AS FileCount
  FROM FolderTree
  ORDER BY folder_path, Nazev;

  2. Everything inside folder 9520 (2024_11_15(25M)):
  SELECT d.xID, d.Nazev, d.Pojmenovani, d.Slozka, d.folder_path, d.Smazano,
         CASE WHEN d.Slozka = 1 THEN 'FOLDER' ELSE 'FILE' END AS Type
  FROM [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_vazby v
  INNER JOIN [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_dokumenty d
      ON v.k_dokumenty = d.xID

    AND d.Smazano IS NULL
  ORDER BY d.Slozka DESC, d.Nazev;

  3. Verify the chain from 9520 back to stavba 28:
  ;WITH chain AS (
      SELECT v.k_dokumenty, v.tabulka_id, v.tabulka, 0 AS depth
      FROM [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_vazby v
      WHERE v.k_dokumenty = 9520 AND v.tabulka = 'd_dokumenty'

      UNION ALL

      SELECT v.k_dokumenty, v.tabulka_id, v.tabulka, c.depth + 1
      FROM [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_vazby v
      INNER JOIN chain c ON v.k_dokumenty = c.tabulka_id AND c.tabulka = 'd_dokumenty'
      WHERE v.tabulka IN ('d_dokumenty', 's_stavby')
  )
  SELECT c.*,
         d.Nazev AS DokumentNazev, d.folder_path,
         s.xID AS StavbaId, s.nazev AS StavbaNazev, s.znacka AS StavbaZnacka
  FROM chain c
  LEFT JOIN [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_dokumenty d ON d.xID = c.tabulka_id AND c.tabulka = 'd_dokumenty'
  LEFT JOIN [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.s_stavby s ON s.xID = c.tabulka_id AND c.tabulka = 's_stavby'
  ORDER BY c.depth;

● -- Find folder '2024_11_15(25M)' and show all its children (files + subfolders)
  SELECT parent.xID AS ParentId, parent.Nazev AS ParentNazev, parent.folder_path AS ParentPath,
         d.xID, d.Nazev, d.Pojmenovani, d.Slozka,
         CASE WHEN d.Slozka = 1 THEN 'FOLDER' ELSE 'FILE' END AS Type,
         d.folder_path, d.Smazano
  FROM [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_dokumenty parent
  INNER JOIN [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_vazby v
      ON v.tabulka_id = parent.xID AND v.tabulka = 'd_dokumenty'
  INNER JOIN [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_dokumenty d
      ON v.k_dokumenty = d.xID
  WHERE parent.Nazev = '2024_11_15(25M)' AND parent.Slozka = 1
  ORDER BY parent.xID, d.Slozka DESC, d.Nazev;

  This finds any folder named 2024_11_15(25M) across all stavby and lists everything inside it — both files and subfolders.

❯ try to find any folders with these names (check is as substring)

● SELECT xID, Nazev, Pojmenovani, Slozka, folder_path, Smazano
  FROM [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_dokumenty
  WHERE Slozka = 1
    AND (Nazev LIKE '%2024_11_15%' OR Nazev LIKE '%25M%')
    AND Smazano IS NULL
  ORDER BY Nazev;

  Note: In SQL _ is a wildcard for a single character in LIKE, so 2024_11_15 will also match things like 2024-11-15 or 2024X11X15.
  If you want an exact underscore match:

  SELECT xID, Nazev, Pojmenovani, Slozka, folder_path, Smazano
  FROM [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_dokumenty
  WHERE Slozka = 1
    AND (Nazev LIKE '%2024[_]11[_]15%' OR Nazev LIKE '%25M%')
    AND Smazano IS NULL
  ORDER BY Nazev;

  [_] escapes the underscore to match it literally.

❯ how is incorporated user Štěpán Verner in that folders tree?

● Good question. In the DMS permissions model, users are connected to folders via permissions (d_dokumenty_opravneni) and/or groups
   (c_uzivatele_skupiny). Let me give you scripts to check both:

  1. Find Štěpán Verner's user ID:
  SELECT xID, jmeno, prijmeni, login, email
  FROM [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.c_uzivatele
  WHERE prijmeni LIKE '%Verner%';

  2. His group memberships:
  SELECT s.xID AS SkupinaId, s.nazev AS SkupinaNazev, vs.k_uzivatel
  FROM [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.c_uzivatele u
  INNER JOIN [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.c_uzivatele_ve_skupine vs
      ON vs.k_uzivatel = u.xID
  INNER JOIN [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.c_uzivatele_skupiny s
      ON s.xID = vs.k_skupina
  WHERE u.prijmeni LIKE '%Verner%';

  3. His direct permissions on folders (stavba 21):
  SELECT d.xID, d.Nazev, d.Slozka, d.folder_path,
         op.can_read AS CanRead, op.can_write AS CanWrite, op.can_modify AS CanModify
  FROM [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_dokumenty_opravneni op
  INNER JOIN [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_dokumenty d
      ON d.xID = op.k_dokumenty
  INNER JOIN [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.c_uzivatele u
      ON u.xID = op.k_uzivatel
  WHERE u.prijmeni LIKE '%Verner%'
    AND d.Slozka = 1
  ORDER BY d.folder_path;

  4. His group permissions on folders (stavba 21):
  SELECT d.xID, d.Nazev, d.Slozka, d.folder_path,
         s.nazev AS SkupinaNazev,
         op.can_read AS CanRead, op.can_write AS CanWrite, op.can_modify AS CanModify
  FROM [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_dokumenty_opravneni op
  INNER JOIN [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_dokumenty d
      ON d.xID = op.k_dokumenty
  INNER JOIN [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.c_uzivatele_skupiny s
      ON s.xID = op.k_skupina
  INNER JOIN [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.c_uzivatele_ve_skupine vs
      ON vs.k_skupina = s.xID
  INNER JOIN [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.c_uzivatele u
      ON u.xID = vs.k_uzivatel
  WHERE u.prijmeni LIKE '%Verner%'
    AND d.Slozka = 1
  ORDER BY d.folder_path;

  Run query 1 first to get his xID, then the rest will show how he's connected to the DMS folder tree — both directly and through
  groups.

  The real bug

  The customer sees 8 subfolders inside 2024_11_15(25M) on their production server. You can't reproduce it because your backup
  doesn't contain those 8 subfolders. Your SQL queries confirmed: folder 9520 has 110 files, 0 subfolders.

  The data discrepancy

  You were assured the backup matches production, but it doesn't. Possible causes:

  1. Backup was taken before those 8 subfolders were created by the customer
  2. Backup was partial/incomplete — some records missing
  3. Different database — the customer may be on a newer version of the data

  Also note: the user email is different — stepan.verner@metrostav.cz (production) vs skrabal@proconom.cz (your test). This
  shouldn't affect folder visibility, but it's worth noting.

  customer when they created those 8 subfolders — if after June 15, that explains everything.
  2. Ask for a fresh backup — request a new DB backup from the customer that includes the current state with those subfolders.
  3. Alternatively, check production directly — if you have read access to their production DB, run the SQL query there:
  SELECT d.xID, d.Nazev, d.Slozka, d.folder_path
  FROM [PRODUCTION_DB_NAME].dbo.d_vazby v
  INNER JOIN [PRODUCTION_DB_NAME].dbo.d_dokumenty d

    AND d.Slozka = 1 AND d.Smazano IS NULL
  ORDER BY d.Nazev;

❯ would be possible to check it via application insights that we have available and we should be able to connect to customer

● Yes, good idea. If Application Insights is logging API requests, we can see what the customer's production API actually returned
  when they expanded that folder.

  The API call for expanding a folder is GetDocumentGroupPermissions with EntityType = SLOZKA and EntityId = the folder's ID.

  KQL query — find the API calls for expanding 2024_11_15(25M):

  requests
  | where timestamp > ago(30d)
  | where url has "DocumentGroupPermissions" or name has "DocumentGroupPermissions"
  | where url has "9520" or url has "SLOZKA"
  | project timestamp, url, resultCode, duration, customDimensions
  | order by timestamp desc
  | take 50

  If the endpoint uses POST (not query string), try looking at traces or custom dimensions:

  requests
  | where timestamp > ago(30d)
  | where name has "DocumentGroupPermissions"
  | project timestamp, name, url, resultCode, duration, customDimensions
  | order by timestamp desc
  | take 50

  Or broader — find any DMS/document permission requests from Verner:

  requests
  | where timestamp > ago(30d)
  | where name has "Document" and name has "Permission"
  | project timestamp, name, url, resultCode, duration, customDimensions
  | order by timestamp desc
  | take 100

  Key questions before we dig deeper:

  1. Do you log request/response bodies in Application Insights, or only URLs + status codes? (If bodies are logged, we can see the
   actual SlozkyPrava list with the 8 subfolders)
  2. Do you have the customer's database name in production? (The folder xID might be different than 9520 on their server)
  3. Can you access their Application Insights via Azure Portal, or do you need me to help with something else?

  If response bodies aren't logged, we can at least confirm the request happened and check if the entity ID matches — that would
  tell us whether the folder ID is the same or different on production.

❯ yes, if it is true that those folders are still in their production database I should be able to find out via application

● Right. When someone expands 2024_11_15(25M) in the permissions tree, the web UI calls the API endpoint
  GetDocumentGroupPermissions with EntityType = SLOZKA and EntityId = that folder's ID.

  Here are KQL queries to try in Application Insights:

  1. Find all calls to the document permissions endpoint:
  requests
  | where timestamp > ago(30d)
  | where name has "DocumentGroupPermissions"
  | project timestamp, name, url, resultCode, duration,
           requestBody = customDimensions["RequestBody"],
           responseBody = customDimensions["ResponseBody"]
  | order by timestamp desc
  | take 100

  2. If request/response bodies aren't logged, check traces instead:
  traces
  | where timestamp > ago(30d)
  | where message has "DocumentGroupPermissions" or message has "9520"
  | project timestamp, message, severityLevel, customDimensions
  | order by timestamp desc
  | take 50

  3. If you know the endpoint URL pattern, search by URL:
  requests
  | where timestamp > ago(30d)
  | where url has "document" and url has "permission"
  | project timestamp, name, url, resultCode, duration
  | order by timestamp desc
  | take 100

  4. Broader — any API call that mentions the folder ID (if it's in the URL or query string):
  requests
  | where timestamp > ago(30d)
  | where url has "9520"
  | project timestamp, name, url, resultCode, duration
  | order by timestamp desc

  Note: The folder ID might be different on production than 9520 in your backup. If you don't find 9520, try searching by the
  endpoint name first (queries 1 or 3) to see what entity IDs are being passed.

❯ give me all sub items (folders or files) of '2024_11_15(25M)' for any stavba and any user

● -- Find all children (folders + files) of any folder named '2024_11_15(25M)'
  SELECT parent.xID AS ParentId, parent.Nazev AS ParentNazev, parent.folder_path AS ParentPath,
         d.xID, d.Nazev, d.Pojmenovani, d.Slozka,
         CASE WHEN d.Slozka = 1 THEN 'FOLDER' ELSE 'FILE' END AS Type,
         d.folder_path, d.Smazano
  FROM [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_dokumenty parent
  INNER JOIN [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_vazby v
      ON v.tabulka_id = parent.xID AND v.tabulka = 'd_dokumenty'
  INNER JOIN [MagistratLiberec-2026-6-15-10-59].dbo.d_dokumenty d
      ON v.k_dokumenty = d.xID
  WHERE parent.Nazev LIKE '%2024[_]11[_]15%' AND parent.Slozka = 1
  ORDER BY parent.xID, d.Slozka DESC, d.Nazev;

  This includes all children — even deleted ones (Smazano IS NOT NULL) — so we can see if those 8 subfolders exist but are
  soft-deleted.

● Good, I can see your fix. Now let me understand the full picture — how the tree is built and why the arrow appears for a folder with no subfolders. Let me look at the key files.

● Searched for 3 patterns, read 3 files (ctrl+o to expand)

● Now I see the flow. The key is FolderPermission.HasChildren (line 15 in FolderPermission.cs) — this controls whether the expand arrow shows. It comes from SlozkaPravaDTO.HasChildren set by
  the API. Let me trace back to the stored procedure to see how HasChildren is calculated.

● Searched for 3 patterns, read 2 files (ctrl+o to expand)

● Found a critical issue. In SlozkaPravaDTO.cs:30:

  .ForMember(d => d.HasChildren, o => o.MapFrom(s => true))

  HasChildren is hardcoded to true for ALL folders! The HasChildResolver exists but isn't wired up. Let me look at the XAML to understand how the tree arrow is controlled and how lazy loading
  works.

● Searched for 1 pattern, read 1 file (ctrl+o to expand)

● Now I have the full picture. Here's the analysis:

  Root Cause

  The bug is in the API, not in the WPF code.

  FILE: TestApi\v1\DTOS\Dokument\SlozkaPravaDTO.cs
  CLASS: SlozkaPravaDTOProfile
  LINE: 30

  .ForMember(d => d.HasChildren, o => o.MapFrom(s => true))  // ← HARDCODED to true!

  HasChildren is always set to true for every folder returned by the API. The XAML then uses this value directly:

  <!-- GroupManagerWindow.xaml:867-868 -->
  <DataTrigger Binding="{Binding HasChildren}" Value="False">
      <Setter TargetName="Expander" Property="Visibility" Value="Hidden" />
  </DataTrigger>

  When HasChildren = true, the expand arrow (Expander) is always visible. When user clicks it, LoadFolderData calls the API to fetch subfolder children. For folder "2024_11_15(25M)"
  (xID=9520), the API returns 0 subfolders (it only has 110 image files), so the tree expands to show nothing.

  The HasChildResolver class already exists at line 36-49 of the same file but is not wired up in the mapping profile.

  Why it works in other databases

  In other databases, folders at this nesting level typically do have subfolders. The hardcoded true accidentally works when folders actually have children. It only breaks for leaf folders
  (folders containing only files, no subfolders) — like "2024_11_15(25M)" in this MagistratLiberec database.

  Fix

  The fix belongs in the API mapping profile — replace the hardcoded true with an actual check. The HasChildResolver approach or a simpler inline check would work. Want me to look at
  GetHasSubfolders to see how the existing resolver works, and then propose the exact fix?

 -- Stavby that have nastaveni but NO objects in r_objekty
  SELECT TOP 10
      s.xID AS StavbaId,
      s.Znacka,
      s.Nazev,
      sdr.xID AS SdruzeniId,
      sdr.Znacka AS SdruzeniZnacka
  FROM [Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33].dbo.s_stavby s
  INNER JOIN [Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33].dbo.s_stavby_nastaveni sn ON sn.k_stavba = s.xID
  LEFT JOIN [Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33].dbo.r_objekty ro ON ro.k_stavba = s.xID
  LEFT JOIN [Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33].dbo.c_sdruzeni sdr ON sdr.k_stavba = s.xID
  WHERE ro.xID IS NULL
  ORDER BY s.xID DESC;

  -- Available partners (zhotovitelé)
  SELECT TOP 20 p.xID AS PartnerId, p.Nazev, p.Znacka
  FROM [Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33].dbo.c_partneri p
  ORDER BY p.xID

  -- Get bank IDs for the test
  SELECT TOP 10 b.xID AS BankaId, b.Nazev, b.banka_kod
  FROM [Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33].dbo.c_banky b
  ORDER BY b.xID

  -- Full picture — inspect sdružení data for a stavba
  -- Once you pick a STAVBA_ID, use these to see the full state before/after each test:

  -- 1. Sdružení header
  SELECT sdr.xID, sdr.Znacka, sdr.Nazev, sdr.k_banka, sdr.k_stavba
  FROM [Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33].dbo.c_sdruzeni sdr
  WHERE sdr.k_stavba = 1268

  -- 2. Sdružení members (společnosti)
  SELECT ss.xID, ss.k_sdruzeni, ss.k_zhotovitel AS PartnerId,
         z.Nazev AS PartnerNazev, ss.procento_def AS Podil, ss.poradi, ss.obdobi
  FROM [Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33].dbo.s_stavby_sdruzeni ss
  INNER JOIN [Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33].dbo.c_sdruzeni sdr ON sdr.xID = ss.k_sdruzeni
  LEFT JOIN [Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33].dbo.c_partneri z ON z.xID = ss.k_zhotovitel
  WHERE sdr.k_stavba = 1268
  ORDER BY ss.poradi

  -- 3. Objects check (should be empty for our test stavba)
  SELECT COUNT(*) AS ObjectCount
  FROM [Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33].dbo.r_objekty
  WHERE k_stavba = 1268

  -- 4. ZP references (relevant for delete-member validation)
  SELECT zp.xID, zp.Sdruzeni, zp.k_sdruzeni, obj.k_stavba
  FROM [Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33].dbo.p_zp zp
  INNER JOIN [Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33].dbo.r_objekty obj ON obj.xID = zp.k_objekt
  WHERE obj.k_stavba = 1268
    AND (zp.Sdruzeni = 1 OR zp.k_sdruzeni IS NOT NULL)


    -- Quick check after each test
  SELECT sdr.xID, sdr.Znacka, sdr.Nazev, sdr.k_banka
  FROM [Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33].dbo.c_sdruzeni sdr
  WHERE sdr.k_stavba = 1268

  SELECT ss.xID, ss.k_zhotovitel, z.Nazev, ss.procento_def, ss.poradi
  FROM [Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33].dbo.s_stavby_sdruzeni ss
  INNER JOIN [Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33].dbo.c_sdruzeni sdr ON sdr.xID = ss.k_sdruzeni
  LEFT JOIN [Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33].dbo.c_partneri z ON z.xID = ss.k_zhotovitel
  WHERE sdr.k_stavba = 1268
  ORDER BY ss.poradi

  ---
  Helper SQL: Find suitable test stavba

  -- Stavby with no objects (rozpocetHasAnyObject = false) and no existing sdružení
  SELECT TOP 10
      s.xID AS StavbaId, s.Znacka, s.Nazev,
      sdr.xID AS SdruzeniId, sdr.Znacka AS SdruzeniZnacka
  FROM [Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33].dbo.s_stavby s
  INNER JOIN [Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33].dbo.s_stavby_nastaveni sn ON sn.k_stavba = s.xID
  LEFT JOIN [Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33].dbo.r_objekty ro ON ro.k_stavba = s.xID
  LEFT JOIN [Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33].dbo.c_sdruzeni sdr ON sdr.k_stavba = s.xID
  WHERE ro.xID IS NULL
  ORDER BY s.xID DESC

  Helper SQL: Get partner + bank IDs

  SELECT TOP 20 p.xID AS PartnerId, p.Nazev, p.Znacka
  FROM [Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33].dbo.c_partneri p
  ORDER BY p.xID

  SELECT TOP 10 b.xID AS BankaId, b.Nazev, b.banka_kod
  FROM [Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33].dbo.c_banky b
  ORDER BY b.xID

  Helper SQL: Full picture for a stavba

  -- 1. Sdružení header
  SELECT sdr.xID, sdr.Znacka, sdr.Nazev, sdr.k_banka, sdr.k_stavba
  FROM [Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33].dbo.c_sdruzeni sdr
  WHERE sdr.k_stavba = 1268

  -- 2. Sdružení members
  SELECT ss.xID, ss.k_sdruzeni, ss.k_zhotovitel AS PartnerId,
         z.Nazev AS PartnerNazev, ss.procento_def AS Podil, ss.poradi, ss.obdobi
  FROM [Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33].dbo.s_stavby_sdruzeni ss
  INNER JOIN [Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33].dbo.c_sdruzeni sdr ON sdr.xID = ss.k_sdruzeni
  LEFT JOIN [Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33].dbo.c_partneri z ON z.xID = ss.k_zhotovitel
  WHERE sdr.k_stavba = 1268
  ORDER BY ss.poradi

  -- 3. Objects check
  SELECT COUNT(*) AS ObjectCount
  FROM [Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33].dbo.r_objekty
  WHERE k_stavba = 1268

  -- 4. ZP references (delete-member validation)
  SELECT zp.xID, zp.Sdruzeni, zp.k_sdruzeni, obj.k_stavba
  FROM [Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33].dbo.p_zp zp
  INNER JOIN [Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33].dbo.r_objekty obj ON obj.xID = zp.k_objekt
  WHERE obj.k_stavba = 1268
    AND (zp.Sdruzeni = 1 OR zp.k_sdruzeni IS NOT NULL)

  ---
  ITEM_STATUS reference

  ┌───────┬─────────┬─────────────────┐
  │ Value │  Enum   │     Meaning     │
  ├───────┼─────────┼─────────────────┤
  │ 0     │ NOTHING │ No change       │
  ├───────┼─────────┼─────────────────┤
  │ 1     │ ADDED   │ New member      │
  ├───────┼─────────┼─────────────────┤
  │ 2     │ UPDATED │ Modified member │
  ├───────┼─────────┼─────────────────┤
  │ 3     │ DELETED │ Remove member   │
  └───────┴─────────┴─────────────────┘

  Key lesson learned

  PUT requires the full payload (obecne, dochazka, rozpocet, zmeny, mereni, prostavenost, fakturace) — not just sdruzeni. The handler runs all
  sections in parallel before sdružení. Sending only sdruzeni with nulls for other sections causes those tasks to fail before sdružení code is
  reached. Solution: GET first → save to /tmp/settings.json → inject sdruzeni with jq → PUT.

  Field name mapping GET → PUT

  ┌──────────────┬───────────────┬───────────┐
  │   Section    │   GET field   │ PUT field │
  ├──────────────┼───────────────┼───────────┤
  │ zmeny        │ seznamPozic   │ pozice    │
  ├──────────────┼───────────────┼───────────┤
  │ mereni       │ seznamPozic   │ pozice    │
  ├──────────────┼───────────────┼───────────┤
  │ prostavenost │ seznamPozic   │ pozice    │
  ├──────────────┼───────────────┼───────────┤
  │ fakturace    │ seznamPodpisu │ pozice    │
  └──────────────┴───────────────┴───────────┘

  All signature items in pozice arrays need "status": 0 (NOTHING) added when passing GET data to PUT.

  with BudgetContext. Banks go into BuildingHelpersContext.

  2. types.ts — Add SdruzeniItem

  export interface SdruzeniItem extends StavbaNastaveniProstavenostSdruzeniSpolecnostDTO {
      status: ITEM_STATUS;
      dummyId: string;
  }

  This follows the same pattern as MeasurementItem.

  3. budgetScene.tsx — Render <SdruzeniSection>

  Add <SdruzeniSection /> below the existing content. The section reads from BudgetContext (budgetSettings.sdruzeni) and
  BuildingHelpersContext (banks, partners). The entire section is disabled (read-only) when budgetSettings.rozpocetHasAnyObject ===

  │                                                               │
  │                                    [Smazat sdružení]          │
  └───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  Business rules (matching WPF desktop):
  - Always visible on the Budget tab
  - Disabled (all fields read-only, add/delete buttons hidden) when rozpocetHasAnyObject === true
  - Značka is required — validation on save
  - Percentage sum must = 100% on save (validated by API handler too)
  - No duplicate partners — filter out already-selected partners from the dropdown
  - Add member: creates item with status: ITEM_STATUS.ADDED, dummyId: crypto.randomUUID()
  - Edit member: sets status: ITEM_STATUS.UPDATED (unless already ADDED)
  - Delete member: if status === ADDED → remove from array; else → mark status: DELETED
  - Partners list comes from SettingsContext.partners
  - Banks list comes from BuildingHelpersContext.buildingHelpersData.banks

  State management: the sdružení header fields (znacka, nazev, bankaId) + member list are managed as local state initialized from
  budgetSettings.sdruzeni. On save, they're mapped to UpdateSdruzeniDTO.

  5. budget/sdruzeniMemberRow.tsx — Member row (NEW)

  Follows measurementItem.tsx pattern:
  - memo wrapped component
  - Uses onBlur + defaultValue for text fields
  - Partner = Autocomplete from MUI (same pattern as other partner selects)
  - Podíl = NumberField
  - Delete = IconButton with DeleteOutlineIcon

      mereni: { ... },
      prostavenost: { ... },
      fakturace: { ... },
      sdruzeni: sdruzeniData,  // ← NEW: mapped from BudgetContext or a new sdruzeni context
  };

  The sdruzeniData is built by mapping the local state to UpdateSdruzeniDTO:
  - id, znacka, nazev, bankaId from header fields
  - smazat: false (unless the "delete sdružení" button was clicked)
  - spolecnosti: mapped from SdruzeniItem[] with their status values

  The changesSame() function in settingsScene.tsx also needs to include sdružení comparison.

  showed stale children after expand. Reverted.

  null). Once real children are loaded, the double-toggle just hides/shows the same stale data.

  Next step: Need a refresh approach that invalidates the lazy-load cache for the specific parent node, or fully reloads the tree

Bug 29923: Desktop - MNG - Nastavení stavby - Změny - lze přidat prázdné řádky zahrnutých osob

"bring back single-click editing on dgPodpisy"

summarization of api changes

● Summary of All Changes

  Problem

  Two bugs when saving "Nastavení stavby" after changing investor type:

  1. FK constraint violation (FK_c_pozice_vazby_c_pozice) — saving crashed because c_pozice_vazby referenced a c_pozice row that was deleted during investor type change
  2. Stale orphan positions — positions created with the "+" button for the OLD investor type survived the cleanup and appeared alongside new positions after save

  ---
  Root Causes

  1. The save loop blindly inserted into c_pozice_vazby using o.KodPozice without checking if that c_pozice row still existed (it was deleted by KopirujPodpisyDleInvestora).
  2. UlozPodpisSUS (called immediately on "+" click) didn't set def_pozice, so it got DB default 1. The cleanup DELETE condition (def_pozice <> 1 OR def_pozice is null) skipped these rows, leaving them as orphans.

  ---
  Changes Made

  All changes are in the API repo (Test_Nove_API). No WPF UI changes were made — WPF files were read-only for understanding the flow.

  ---
  File 1: CustomUsernamePasswordAuth.Service\Service1.cs

  3 edits in method UlozNastaveniStavby:

  ┌─────┬────────────────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │  #  │                  Location                  │                                                                                        What changed                                                                                        │
  ├─────┼────────────────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ 1   │ OsobyPodpisyZBV loop (~line 2853)          │ Added existence check: SELECT xID FROM c_pozice WHERE xID = @kod. If position exists → UPDATE name. If deleted → INSERT new c_pozice with correct typ_investor and def_pozice = 0, use new │
  │     │                                            │  ID for c_pozice_vazby.                                                                                                                                                                    │
  ├─────┼────────────────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ 2   │ OsobyMereni loop (~line 2882)              │ Same pattern as #1, also preserving the popis column.                                                                                                                                      │
  ├─────┼────────────────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ 3   │ After KopirujPodpisyDleInvestora (~line    │ Added orphan cleanup: DELETE c_pozice rows (and their children via k_pozice_stavba) that belong to the OLD typ_investor and have no c_pozice_vazby references.                             │
  │     │ 2702)                                      │                                                                                                                                                                                            │
  └─────┴────────────────────────────────────────────┴────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  ---
  File 2: CustomUsernamePasswordAuth.Service\ZBVBussiness.cs

  10 edits in method UlozPodpisSUS:

  ┌──────┬─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │  #   │                                            What changed                                             │

  │ 1–3  │ 3 stavba-level UlozData("c_pozice", ...) calls — added "def_pozice" column with value 0             │
  ├──────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ 4–10 │ 7 child-level (objekt/ZBV) UlozData("c_pozice", ...) calls — added "def_pozice" column with value 0 │
  └──────┴─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  This ensures all positions created via the "+" button are marked def_pozice = 0, so the investor-change cleanup DELETE can properly remove them.

  ---
  What was NOT changed (and why)

  - WPF UI — no changes needed; the bugs were entirely in the API save logic
  - Web UI endpoint (ManagerStavebController.UpdateNastaveniStavby) — completely separate code path, not affected
  - New stavba creation / CenováDB import paths — OsobyPodpisyZBV is null in those flows, so the changed loops are never reached

  How to test

  1. Open a stavba → Nastavení → pick investor type (e.g. "Správa a údržba silnic")
  2. Go to ZMĚNY tab, add positions with "+" button (e.g. "s1")
  3. Go back to OBECNÉ, change investor type (e.g. to "Technická zpráva komunikací")
  4. Go to ZMĚNY, add new positions (e.g. "s2")
  5. Click Uložit
  6. Expected: Only "s2" positions appear. No crash, no "s1" leftovers.

https://tfs-server.proconom.local/ProconomSW/PROCONOM/_workitems/edit/29727/
Bug 29727: Desktop- DMS- seřazení sloupce Verze se nechová jak má


Shrnutí pro Peťu (CZ)

  Co je za bug?

  Řazení v DMS nefunguje správně — sestupné řazení u VŠECH sloupců (nejen Verze), protože FileComparer při řazení čte zastaralé hodnoty.

  Proč to vzniklo?

  Monika v dubnu 2025 (commit 2fe896526 — "vraceci slozka se radi nahoru") přepsala logiku řazení. Původní kód (David Šeda, 2022) předával směr
  a název sloupce přímo do compareru. Monika to nahradila statickými poli, které se nastavují v eventu SortColumnsChanged — ten ale spouští až
  PO dokončení řazení. Takže comparer při řazení čte hodnoty z předchozího kliknutí.

  Proč si toho nikdo nevšiml?

  - První kliknutí (vzestupně): comparer nemá žádný název sloupce → vrací 0 pro všechno → zachová se původní pořadí → vypadá to správně náhodou
  - Druhé kliknutí (sestupně): comparer teď má správný sloupec, ale směr je stále "vzestupně" z předchozího kliknutí → seřadí vzestupně místo
  sestupně
  - Uživatelé typicky řadí podle názvu vzestupně a jiné sloupce/směry skoro netestují

  Fix (3 změny)

  1. XAML: SortColumnsChanged → SortColumnsChanging (spouští se PŘED řazením)
  2. Code-behind: handler čte z e.AddedItems místo z SortColumnDescriptions
  3. FileComparer: odstraněn řádek, který přepisoval správnou hodnotu od Syncfusion zastaralou statickou hodnotou

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  ---
  Step 2 — New file: Bulk Update Dialog

  FILE: src/todo/components/bulkUpdateDialog.tsx (NEW)

  This is a MUI Dialog (not Syncfusion — per your rules) that shows:

  ┌──────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────────┐
  │            Field             │        Component         │               Behavior                │
  ├──────────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Stav (state)                 │ MUI Select dropdown      │ Optional — leave empty = don't change │
  ├──────────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Deadline                     │ MUI DatePicker           │ Optional — leave empty = don't change │
  ├──────────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Přidat štítky (add tags)     │ MUI Autocomplete (multi) │ Select from all available tags        │
  ├──────────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Odebrat štítky (remove tags) │ MUI Autocomplete (multi) │ Select from all available tags        │
  └──────────────────────────────┴──────────────────────────┴───────────────────────────────────────┘

  WPF analogy: This is like a Window with a ComboBox, a DatePicker, and two ListBox controls with multi-select. The dialog has OK/Cancel buttons. When the user clicks OK, the dialog returns the selected values back to the caller —
  similar to DialogResult in WPF.

  In React terms:
  - The dialog is a component that receives props: open (boolean), onClose (callback), tags (available tags), states (available states)
  - When user clicks "Uložit" (Save), it calls onClose with the form data
  - When user clicks "Zrušit" (Cancel), it calls onClose(null)
  - The caller awaits the result using the useShowDialog hook

  ---
  Step 3 — New file: Bulk Update hook

  FILE: src/todo/hooks/useBulkUpdateTodos.ts (NEW)

  A react-query mutation hook that:
  1. Creates the authenticated API client
  2. Calls the NSwag-generated toDoList_BulkUpdateToDos() method
  3. On success: invalidates the todo list cache, shows success toast
  4. On error: shows error toast
  5. Returns failedTodos for the FailedTodosDialog

  WPF analogy: This is like a service method wrapped in a BackgroundWorker — it handles the async API call, error handling, and UI updates after completion. In React, a "hook" is a reusable function that manages state and side effects.

  ---
  Step 4 — Edit existing file: todoListGrid.tsx

  FILE: src/todo/components/todoListGrid.tsx

  Changes:
  1. Add "Bulk Update" button to the toolbar (next to the existing "Bulk Delete" button)
    - Icon: EditIcon from MUI
    - Tooltip: t('Hromadná úprava') (localized)
    - Visible when: at least 1 unlocked ToDo is selected
    - Disabled when: all selected ToDos are locked
  2. Add click handler handleBulkUpdate:
    - Gets selected records: gridRef.current?.getSelectedRecords() as ToDoDTO[]
    - Checks if ALL selected are locked → show info message ("Všechny ToDo jsou uzamknuty"), return
    - Checks if SOME are locked → show info dialog listing locked ones (ID + name) → user confirms to continue
    - Opens the BulkUpdateDialog
    - On dialog result → calls the bulk update hook
    - On response → shows FailedTodosDialog if any failed, refreshes grid

  WPF analogy: This is like adding a new Button to a ToolBar in XAML, with a Command binding that checks CanExecute (disabled when no selection or all locked) and an Execute method that opens a dialog and processes the result.

  Step 5 — Edit existing file: useTodoList.ts

  FILE: src/todo/hooks/useTodoList.ts

  Add the bulk update mutation function (or import from the new hook). Follow the exact same pattern as the existing deleteTodo function.

  Flow Diagram

  User selects ToDos in grid (Shift/Ctrl)
      ↓
  Clicks "Hromadná úprava" button
      ↓
  handleBulkUpdate() checks selection:
      ├── ALL locked → info toast "Všechny ToDo jsou uzamknuty" → STOP
      ├── SOME locked → info dialog listing locked ones → user confirms
      └── NONE locked → proceed directly
      ↓
  Opens BulkUpdateDialog (stav, deadline, tags add/remove)
      ↓
  User fills in changes → clicks "Uložit"
      ↓
  useBulkUpdateTodos hook calls API: PUT /api/todolist/ukoly/bulk
      ↓
  API response:
      ├── updatedCount > 0 → success toast "X úkolů upraveno"
      ├── failedTodos.length > 0 → show FailedTodosDialog
      └── Invalidate cache → grid refreshes automatically

Bulk ToDo Update Feature — API Summary

  Endpoint

  PUT /api/todolist/ukoly/bulk

  Accepts a JSON body with:
  - ukolIds — list of ToDo IDs to update
  - kStav — (optional) new state ID
  - deadline — (optional) new deadline date
  - stitkyPridat — (optional) list of tag IDs to add
  - stitkyOdebrat — (optional) list of tag IDs to remove

  Only applies changes that are actually different per ToDo. Returns updatedCount and failedTodos (locked ToDos are skipped with a reason).

  └─────┴────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┴───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  ---
  Handler Logic (BulkUpdateToDosHandler.Handle)

  1. Validates request (non-empty IDs, deadline not in the past)
  2. Pre-loads tag names and state names for history/notifications
  3. Loads all requested ToDos with their tag bindings and creator navigation
  4. Iterates each ToDo:
    - Locked → skipped, added to failedTodos
    - Stav → if different from current, updates + writes history record
    - Deadline → if different, updates + writes history + schedules/reschedules deadline reminder via TodoEmailHelper
    - Tags to add → skips already assigned, creates ToDoUkolyStitkyVazby + history
    - Tags to remove → skips if not assigned, removes binding + history
    - Only counts as changed if at least one field was actually modified
  5. Saves all changes in one SaveChanges() call
  6. Sends notifications — one email per ToDo per affected user (responsible users + creator if not already included)

  Email Notification Flow

  Handler.SendNotifications()
    → Resolves UzelDTO (stavba context for the email)
    → Gets "Přejít na úkol" link
    → Gets recipients: GetOdpovedne() + creator
    → For each recipient:
        → Creates ToDoBulkUpdatedNotificationModel
        → Factory renders Razor template → EmailMessage
        → EmailMessage.Check() verifies user has NotifikaceMail=true + valid email
        → EmailService.Send() → HTTP POST to Azure Email Gate Service
            → Gate service queues → SmtpClient sends via configured SMTP

  Decisions

  ┌───────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │     Topic     │                            Decision                            │
  ├───────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Deadline      │ Only set new value, null = don't change, no clearing           │
  ├───────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Notifications │ 1 email per ToDo per affected user (creator + odpovědné osoby) │
  ├───────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Concurrency   │ Skip CasZnamka (same pattern as batch delete)                  │
  ├───────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Permissions   │ No creator check, only Uzamceno blocks                         │
  ├───────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Tags          │ Show all tags, no filtering in API                             │
  ├───────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Locked ToDos  │ Skipped, returned in failedTodos with reason                   │
  ├───────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Localization  │ Hardcoded Czech (same pattern as all other ToDo emails)        │
  └───────────────┴────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  ---
  Today's Debugging Session

  The email wasn't arriving during earlier testing. Root cause: user ID 69 didn't exist in the database being tested against. SingleOrDefault returned null → empty recipients list → zero emails sent — completely silent. We confirmed
  this by:

  1. Adding diagnostic Console.WriteLine logging to trace every step
  2. Testing via curl against the correct database (Prerelease-2026-5-22-10-33)
  3. Verified the full flow worked end-to-end
  4. Removed diagnostics and reverted to real recipients logic

Summary — Bulk ToDo Update Feature (API)

  New endpoint

  PUT /api/todolist/ukoly/bulk

  Accepts a list of ToDo IDs and optionally: new Stav, new Deadline, tags to add, tags to remove. Only applies changes that are actually different per ToDo. Locked ToDos are
  skipped and reported in failedTodos. History is logged per field per ToDo. Notification email sent per ToDo to affected users.

https://tfs-server.proconom.local/ProconomSW/PROCONOM/_workitems/edit/29688/
Bug 29688: Desktop - měření - gridové zobrazení - rozkliknutí SD v gridovém zobrazení a proconom spadne bez hlášky

revert the UpdaterServer temporary changes



● The Bug

  FILE: Proconom\eConoM\ProconomSections\MereniSectionSeznam.xaml.cs
  METHOD: LoadDataPolozka
  LINES: 393 vs 395

  391:  foreach (MereniUzelDC polozka in polozky)
  392:  {
  393:      bool uzamceno = polozka.Mereni.Any(p => p.Uzamceno);  // ← uses Mereni HERE
  394:
  395:      if (polozka.Mereni == null)   // ← checks null AFTER — too late
  396:      {
  397:          return;
  398:      }

  The code checks if polozka.Mereni is null on line 395, but it already accesses it on line 393 with .Any(). If any položka under SD "zemní práce" has Mereni == null, it crashes on line 393
  with a NullReferenceException before the null check ever runs.

  Why you don't see the exception: The call chain is:
  1. ExpandCollapse (line 634, async Task)
  2. → calls LoadDataPolozka (line 660)
  3. → NullReferenceException thrown on line 393

  Because ExpandCollapse is async Task and is likely called without await or via a fire-and-forget event handler, the exception gets swallowed by the Task — it never bubbles up to the UI, no
  error dialog appears, the app just silently fails and the grid stops responding (looks like a crash/freeze).

  Proposed Fix

  Move the null check before the .Any() call, and change return to continue (so one bad položka doesn't skip all remaining ones):

  foreach (MereniUzelDC polozka in polozky)
  {
      if (polozka.Mereni == null)
      {
          continue;
      }

      bool uzamceno = polozka.Mereni.Any(p => p.Uzamceno);
      // ... rest stays the same

  CHANGED:
  - Null check moved from line 395 to before line 393
  - return → continue (skip this one položka, don't abort the entire loop)

  INTENTIONALLY UNCHANGED:
  - Everything else in the method stays as-is — the .Any() call, the data mapping, the ZBV logic — none of it is affected

  How to Test

  Since the exception is swallowed, you can't rely on the debugger catching it. Two approaches:

  Approach 1 — Add a temporary breakpoint

  1. Put a breakpoint on the new continue line
  2. Open Měření grid view, expand stavba Azir / školení 11 → any SO → SD "zemní práce"
  3. If the breakpoint hits → you confirmed Mereni was null, and the fix prevented the crash
  4. The grid should now load (possibly with some položky missing — the ones with null Mereni)

  Approach 2 — Check the data

  Let me query the DB to see if any položka under "zemní práce" actually has null Mereni data. This would confirm the root cause.

  Want me to apply the fix and also check the database for the "zemní práce" SD data?

Bug 29698: Desktop - měření - Hromadní záznam stavby/objektu - checkbox "Zahrnout pouze uzamčené záznamy" nefunguje

Summary — BUG 29698

  Bug: "Zahrnout pouze uzamčené záznamy" checkbox in Hromadný záznam stavby/objektu print had no effect — all measured items were printed regardless.

  Root cause: The initial LINQ query selecting položky to print (line 103) did not filter by lock status. The PouzeZamknute flag was only used deeper in the stored procedure to filter čerpání rows within
  each položka — but the položka itself was always included, producing empty pages for unlocked items.

  Fix: Added && (!request.PouzeZamknute || ssdPol.Uzamceno) to the where clause.

  FILE: Test_Nove_API\TiskReportApi\Modules\Reports\Handlers\GetHromadnyZaznamMereniDataHandler.cs
  CLASS: GetHromadnyZaznamMereniReportHandler
  METHOD: Handle
  LINE: 104

  One line added, no other files changed.

Feature 28110: ESD: našeptávač u mechanizace a pracovníka [CS-271]

obnovit z paměti :
"Continue feature 28110 — ESD whispering for workers and machines, WPF side"

Complete Testing Guide — ESD Whispering Endpoints

  SQL — Find StavbaId with data

  SELECT TOP 10
      s.xID AS StavbaId,
      s.znacka,
      s.nazev,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [Prerelease-2026-5-12-8-54].dbo.s_sd_lide l
       INNER JOIN [Prerelease-2026-5-12-8-54].dbo.s_stav_denik sd ON l.k_stav_denik = sd.xID
       WHERE sd.k_stavba = s.xID AND l.typ IS NOT NULL AND l.typ != '') AS WorkerCount,
      (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT l.funkce) FROM [Prerelease-2026-5-12-8-54].dbo.s_sd_lide l
       INNER JOIN [Prerelease-2026-5-12-8-54].dbo.s_stav_denik sd ON l.k_stav_denik = sd.xID
       WHERE sd.k_stavba = s.xID AND l.funkce IS NOT NULL AND l.funkce != '') AS FunctionCount,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [Prerelease-2026-5-12-8-54].dbo.s_sd_stroje st
       INNER JOIN [Prerelease-2026-5-12-8-54].dbo.s_stav_denik sd ON st.k_stav_denik = sd.xID
       WHERE sd.k_stavba = s.xID AND st.typ IS NOT NULL AND st.typ != '') AS MachineCount
  FROM [Prerelease-2026-5-12-8-54].dbo.s_stavby s
  WHERE EXISTS (
      SELECT 1 FROM [Prerelease-2026-5-12-8-54].dbo.s_stav_denik sd
      WHERE sd.k_stavba = s.xID
  )
  ORDER BY WorkerCount DESC

  Curl — Complete flow (Git Bash, 127.0.0.1)

  # ============================================
  # STEP 1: Login
  # ============================================
  PREAUTH=$(curl -sk -X POST https://127.0.0.1:44301/api/anonym/auth/authenticate/master \
    -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
    -d '{"userName":"skrabal@proconom.cz","password":"YOUR_PASSWORD","idp":1}' | jq -r '.authToken')

  echo "PREAUTH: $PREAUTH"

  # ============================================
  # STEP 2: Database token (prerelease)
  # ============================================
  TOKEN=$(curl -sk -X POST https://127.0.0.1:44301/api/auth/databasetoken \
    -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
    -H "Authorization: Bearer $PREAUTH" \
    -d '{"database":"Prerelease-2026-5-12-8-54"}' | jq -r '.uzivatel.token')

  echo "TOKEN: $TOKEN"

  # ============================================
  # STEP 3: Baseline — existing workerFunctions
  # ============================================
  curl -sk -X GET "https://127.0.0.1:44301/api/stavebnidenik/stavby/1/workerFunctions" \
    -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" | jq .

  # ============================================
  # STEP 4: NEW — workerNames
  # ============================================
  curl -sk -X GET "https://127.0.0.1:44301/api/stavebnidenik/stavby/1/workerNames" \
    -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" | jq .

  # ============================================
  # STEP 5: NEW — machineNames
  # ============================================
  curl -sk -X GET "https://127.0.0.1:44301/api/stavebnidenik/stavby/1/machineNames" \
    -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" | jq .

  # ============================================
  # STEP 6: With optional ObjektID filter
  # ============================================
  curl -sk -X GET "https://127.0.0.1:44301/api/stavebnidenik/stavby/1/workerNames?ObjektID=239" \
    -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" | jq .

  curl -sk -X GET "https://127.0.0.1:44301/api/stavebnidenik/stavby/1/machineNames?ObjektID=239" \
    -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" | jq .

  # ============================================
  # STEP 7: Error cases
  # ============================================
  # Invalid StavbaId → expect 400
  curl -sk -X GET "https://127.0.0.1:44301/api/stavebnidenik/stavby/999999/workerNames" \
    -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" | jq .

  # No auth → expect 401
  curl -sk -X GET "https://127.0.0.1:44301/api/stavebnidenik/stavby/1/workerNames" | jq .

Bug 29640: desktop - wf - chybí posuvníky na jednotlivé procesy

Summary — BUG 29640 fix

  Two changes in WorkFlowReporting.xaml / .xaml.cs:

  1. XAML — line 1265: Changed ScrollViewer.HorizontalScrollBarVisibility from "Auto" to "Disabled" on the outer lvHigh ListView.
  This stops the outer ScrollViewer from giving infinite horizontal space to cards, forcing each process card to fit the viewport
  width. The inner per-process ListView (already set to "Auto") now correctly shows a horizontal scrollbar when steps overflow.

  2. Code-behind — line 481: Added a ScrollBar parent check in UlohyListView_PreviewMouseLeftButtonDown. The handler was setting
  e.Handled = true for any click that didn't hit a ListViewItem — including clicks on the scrollbar. The new check lets scrollbar
  clicks pass through.

Feature 28057: todo - hromadný export todo do pdf

Summary — Task 28057: Bulk ToDo PDF Export (API only)

  What was implemented

  A new API endpoint that accepts a list of ToDo IDs and returns a single merged PDF containing all selected ToDo detail reports
  (without DMS attachments).

  Files changed

  ┌─────┬──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┬──────┬─────────────────────────┐
  │  #  │                                         File                                         │ Type │         Change          │
  ├─────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┼──────┼─────────────────────────┤
  │ 1   │ Test_Nove_API\TiskReportApi\Modules\Reports\Handlers\GetToDoDetailBulkDataHandler.cs │ NEW  │ Handler + request class │
  ├─────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┼──────┼─────────────────────────┤
  │ 2   │ Test_Nove_API\TiskReportApi\Modules\Reports\ReportsController.cs:651-654             │ EDIT │ Added endpoint route    │
  └─────┴──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┴──────┴─────────────────────────┘

  Endpoint

  - URL: POST api/Reports/ToDoDetailBulk/data
  - Body: { "toDoIds": [1863, 1862, 695] }
  - Returns: Single merged PDF (ToDo-Export.pdf, application/octet-stream)

  How it works

  1. Receives List<int> ToDoIds via [FromBody]
  2. Loops over each ID, creates ReportToDoDetail_PDF with TiskZDokumentu = false (no DMS docs)
  3. Merges individual PDFs via PdfDocumentBase.Merge() (same pattern as GetRekapitulaceCerpaniPolozekHandler)
  4. Returns single merged PDF stream

  What's untouched

  - Existing GET api/Reports/ToDoDetail/data — zero changes
  - ReportToDoDetail_PDF.cs — zero changes
  - PdfReportHelper.cs — zero changes
  - GetToDoDetailDataHandler.cs — zero changes
  - No DB changes, no permission changes

  Tested via curl

  - Port 5014 (TiskReportApi, HTTP) — confirmed 200 OK with valid merged PDFs
  - Tested with multiple real ToDo IDs (1863, 1862, 695, 1861)

  Remaining (WPF UI — deferred)

  - NSwag regeneration
  - IReportServer.cs / ReportServer.cs — bulk method
  - IsolatedStorageBC.cs — bump grid storage key
  - ToDoList.xaml — SelectionMode="Extended" + checkbox column
  - ToDoList.xaml.cs — print button enabled when ≥1 selected, attachments dropdown disabled when >1, branching logic (single =
  existing, bulk = new endpoint)

--------------- curl testing ---------------------------------------

# Step 1: Login
  PREAUTH=$(curl -sk -X POST https://localhost:44301/api/anonym/auth/authenticate/master \
    -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
    -d '{"userName":"skrabal@proconom.cz","password":"YOUR_PASSWORD","idp":1}' | jq -r '.authToken')
  echo "PREAUTH=$PREAUTH"

  # Step 2: Database token
  TOKEN=$(curl -sk -X POST https://localhost:44301/api/auth/databasetoken \
    -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
    -H "Authorization: Bearer $PREAUTH" \
    -d '{"database":"Data_Develop"}' | jq -r '.uzivatel.token')
  echo "TOKEN=$TOKEN"

  # Step 3: Bulk ToDo PDF export
  curl -v -X POST "http://localhost:5014/api/Reports/ToDoDetailBulk/data" \
    -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
    -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" \
    -d '{"toDoIds":[1863,1862,695]}' \
    -o ToDo-Export.pdf

  Notes:
  - Auth goes to https://localhost:44301 (TestApi, HTTPS)
  - Bulk endpoint goes to http://localhost:5014 (TiskReportApi, HTTP)
  - Replace YOUR_PASSWORD, Data_Develop with real password and database
  - Replace ToDo IDs with valid ones from your DB

Summary of Fix — Task 28318

  Problem: In the "Nastavení skupin" window, when adding a new user to a group (e.g. "Skupina - Skupina"), the user picker popup ("Uživatel")
  showed ALL users instead of only those with rights to the selected construction ("Ukázková - Stavba").

  Root cause: Broken WPF binding. The CiselnikButtonBox at line 1193 of GroupManagerWindow.xaml was inside a DataTemplate with DataType=User, so
  {Binding ConstructionId} resolved against the User object — which has no such property. The binding silently failed, Kod_Stavba stayed null,

  ┌────────────────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐

  │ ConstructionId}"                   │ AncestorType=Window}}"                                                                              │
  └────────────────────────────────────┴─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  What it fixes: Both the inline autocomplete search and the full dialog window (CiselnikBaseWindow → ucCisUzivatele) now receive the correct
  construction ID, which flows to GetAllUzivateleHandler where CUzivateleStavbyPristup filtering is already implemented.

Task 28318 — Summary

  Bug: In the GroupManager dialog ("Nastavení skupin"), all document permission groups — including the root "Všichni" group —
  showed users who were denied access to the selected construction. No filtering against the CUzivateleStavbyPristup deny list was
  applied.

  Root cause: GetDocumentGroupsByConstructionHandler loaded groups with users via a plain EF Include(CUzivateleVazbySkupiny)
  without checking construction access rights.

  Fix: Added an optional query parameter ExcludeDeniedUsers (default false) to the existing GET api/dokumenty/group endpoint. When
  true, the handler queries the deny list and removes denied users from all groups before returning. WPF passes true, web UI
  doesn't pass it — zero impact on existing consumers.

  Files changed

  API (1 file):
  - Test_Nove_API\TestApi\v1\Modules\Dokumenty\Handlers\DocumentGroup\GetDocumentGroupsByConstructionHandler.cs — added
  ExcludeDeniedUsers property to request + post-processing filter in handler

  WPF (4 files):
  - Proconom\ProconomApi\ProconomApiClient.cs — NSwag regenerated
  - Proconom\eConoM\Servers\GroupManagerServer\IGroupManagerServer.cs — added optional param to interface
  - Proconom\eConoM\Servers\GroupManagerServer\GroupManagerServer.cs — passes param to NSwag client
  - Proconom\eConoM\Moduly\Admin\GroupManager\GroupManagerContext.cs:344 — passes excludeDeniedUsers: true

  Related: User Story 23779 (closed) solved the same problem for the web UI using a different approach. Our fix reuses the existing
   endpoint with backward-compatible opt-in filtering.

https://tfs-server.proconom.local/ProconomSW/PROCONOM/_workitems/edit/29508/
Bug 29508: Desktop - ESD - deník objektu - po překliknutí dne se vrátím na hlavní esd

Summary — Bug #29508 Fix

  FILE: Proconom\eConoM\ProconomSections\StavDenikSectionControl.xaml.cs
  CLASS: StavDenikSectionControl

  Bug: In ESD diary, when changing the date (< / > arrows), the tree selection jumped to the root (hlavní deník stavby) instead of staying on the
  selected object diary. Data also showed stale content from the previous date.

  Root cause: NactiStrom() always selected the root node, AND data reloading relied entirely on fragile event handler chains
  (tvDenik_SelectedItemChanged → Refresh()) with async race conditions.

  ---
  4 changes made (diff against prerelease):

  1. _hlavniForm_DateSelected — Save selected item before rebuild (lines 462-469)
  Captures reloadObjectId and reloadPopisSO from the currently selected tree item BEFORE the tree is cleared. Root → null (loads hlavní deník), child
   → child's Kod (loads object data).

  2. _hlavniForm_DateSelected — Suppress spurious SelectedItemChanged (lines 472-474)
  Wraps NactiStrom() with isSelectionBeingChanged = true/false. Prevents tvDenik.Items.Clear() from triggering tvDenik_SelectedItemChanged which
  would start a competing async Refresh() with wrong data.

  3. _hlavniForm_DateSelected — Explicit data reload (line 515)
  await PrenactiData(reloadObjectId, reloadPopisSO) — deterministic data load for the new date, independent of event handler timing. No more reliance
   on async event chains.

  4. NactiStrom — Handle root re-selection (lines 1762-1766)
  Changed if (ObjectToSelect == null) to if (ObjectToSelect == null || (!rozpocet && ObjectToSelect.Value == DataStavba.Id)). When the root itself
  was selected, it gets re-selected and ObjectToSelect is cleared. Previously root was never re-selected because ObjectToSelect was set to root's
  Kod.

  5. PrenactiData — Move DataContext null earlier (line 692)
  Moved this.DataContext = null from after the first await to before it. Prevents 2-3 seconds of stale data display during API call delay. Also
  avoids breaking the _hlavniForm_DateSelected entry guard on rapid date changes.

---------------------------------------


Summary — Bug #29508

  Bug: V ESD deníku, při překliknutí dne (šipkou < / >), se výběr v levém stromě vždy přeskočil na hlavní deník stavby, místo aby zůstal na vybraném
  objektovém deníku.

  Root cause: NactiStrom() na řádku 1749 vždy nastavil stavba.IsSelected = true na kořenový uzel stromu — i když uživatel stál na podřízeném objektu.
   Mechanismus rememberPath() / ObjectToSelect sice existoval a zapamatoval si vybraný objekt, ale selekce kořene se spustila dřív, než se strom
  stihl expandovat a vybrat správný potomek.

  Fix: Podmínka if (ObjectToSelect == null) před stavba.IsSelected = true — kořen se vybere jen pokud žádný potomek nečeká na výběr.

  FILE: eConoM\ProconomSections\StavDenikSectionControl.xaml.cs
  METHOD: NactiStrom()
  LINE: 1749
