https://tfs-server.proconom.local/ProconomSW/PROCONOM/_workitems/edit/29498/
Desktop- WF- přidala jsem do diskuze obrázek z DMS a následně ho v DMS nalistovala-> 2x šipka zpět

netestováné !!!!!

OK, I understand now. Here's my analysis and proposed fix.

  Root Cause

  The DMS grid has two mechanisms for showing the ".." back arrow:
  1. UnBoundRow at position "Top" (row index 1) — the "proper" back arrow at the top
  2. Data row from FolderContent — the actual ".." item in the data source

  The CollapseNavUpRow method is supposed to hide the data row (since the UnBoundRow handles it). But it only checks e.RowIndex == 2, assuming ".."
  is always the first data row. When the grid is sorted in descending order, ".." ends up at the BOTTOM of the list, and CollapseNavUpRow doesn't
  catch it.

  FILE: Proconom\eConoM\Widgety\Dokumenty\DokumentyWidgetControl.xaml.cs
  METHOD: CollapseNavUpRow
  LINE: 6009

  Current code:
  private void CollapseNavUpRow(SfDataGrid dataGrid, QueryRowHeightEventArgs e)
  {
      if (dataGrid is null)
          return;
      DFileDC navUpItem = GetNavUpItem(dataGrid);
      DFileDC viewedNavUpItem = GetViewedNavUpItem(dataGrid);
      bool noNavUpItem = navUpItem is null && viewedNavUpItem is null;
      if (e.RowIndex == 1 && noNavUpItem)
      {
          CollapseRow(e);
          return;
      }
      bool navUpItemIsFilteredOut = navUpItem is null || viewedNavUpItem is null;
      if (e.RowIndex == 2 && !navUpItemIsFilteredOut)  // ← BUG: only checks row 2
      {
          CollapseRow(e);
          return;
      }
  }

  Proposed Fix

  Replace the hardcoded e.RowIndex == 2 check with logic that identifies the ".." row at any position:

  private void CollapseNavUpRow(SfDataGrid dataGrid, QueryRowHeightEventArgs e)
  {
      if (dataGrid is null)
          return;
      DFileDC navUpItem = GetNavUpItem(dataGrid);

      // Collapse UnBoundRow when no ".." item in source
      if (e.RowIndex == 1 && navUpItem is null)
      {
          CollapseRow(e);
          return;
      }

      // Collapse the ".." data row wherever it appears (could be sorted to any position)
      if (navUpItem is not null && e.RowIndex >= 2 && !dataGrid.IsUnBoundRow(e.RowIndex))
      {
          object record = dataGrid.GetRecordAtRowIndex(e.RowIndex);
          if (record is DFileDC file && file.Nazev == "..")
          {
              CollapseRow(e);
              return;
          }
      }
  }

  Key changes:
  - Instead of only checking row index 2, checks ALL data rows (e.RowIndex >= 2)
  - Uses GetRecordAtRowIndex (already used elsewhere in this file, e.g., line 1986) to identify the actual data at each row
  - Collapses the row only if it contains the ".." item
  - Simplified UnBoundRow check: only depends on navUpItem (from raw ItemsSource), not viewedNavUpItem

  INTENTIONALLY UNCHANGED:
  - GetNavUpItem / GetViewedNavUpItem methods — still useful for the UnBoundRow data population in LvDokumentySlozky_QueryUnboundRowInfo
  - AddReturn() logic — the ".." item still needs to exist in FolderContent for navigation to work
  - FileComparer — the * direction logic there is correct for keeping folders/".." pinned when the grid handles sort reversal; the real issue is the
  collapse logic

https://tfs-server.proconom.local/ProconomSW/PROCONOM/_workitems/edit/29464/
desktop - docházka - při úpravě stávajícího záznamu musím znovu vyplnit projekt

Summary — Bug 29464 (tester feedback: project must show immediately)

  Problem

  When editing an existing dochazka record, the PROJEKT field was empty for ~1 second, then the project name appeared. The delay was caused by CiselnikButtonBox making an async API call (NactiPopisDB
  → VratPopisZCiselnikuAsync) to resolve the project name every time the window opened.

  Root Cause

  Two code paths in CiselnikButtonBox triggered NactiPopisDB during window initialization:
  1. OnKod_CiselnikPropertyChanged — when Kod_Ciselnik binding evaluated
  2. UserControl_DataContextChanged — when DataContext was set (catch-all fallback)

  Both are async API calls, causing a visible delay before the text appeared.

  Solution (WPF-only, 2 files)

  FILE: Proconom\eConoM\Ciselniky\CiselnikButtonBox.xaml.cs
  - Added public bool SuppressPopisLoad { get; set; } (defaults to false, no impact on existing usage)
  - Guard in OnKod_CiselnikPropertyChanged (~line 83): skip NactiPopisDB when flag is true
  - Guard in UserControl_DataContextChanged catch-all (~line 858): same

  FILE: Proconom\eConoM\Stavby\Detaily\DochazkaEditControl.xaml.cs
  - DochazkaRecordDCCWithProjects constructor (~line 190): KodStavba set immediately, LoadStavby removed (unused Stavby property, no XAML binding)
  - Edit constructor (~line 475-484): before DataContext assignment, sets SuppressPopisLoad = true, pre-sets Kod_Ciselnik and CiselnikPopis using data already available (data.KodStavba,
  data.NazevStavba), then sets DataContext, then resets flag to false

  Flow After Fix

  1. Window opens → Kod_Ciselnik and text pre-set directly → project name visible instantly
  2. DataContext assigned → both NactiPopisDB code paths blocked by flag → no API call
  3. Binding evaluates → Kod_Ciselnik already has same value → no callback fires
  4. Flag reset → CiselnikButtonBox works normally for any subsequent user interaction (changing project, etc.)

  What Was NOT Changed

  - API (GetUserResourceReportHandler.cs) — reverted, no change
  - XAML (DochazkaEditControl.xaml) — no change, Kod_Ciselnik binding stays
  - New record constructor DochazkaEditControl(int userId) — no change, no project to pre-set
  - All other CiselnikButtonBox instances across the app — SuppressPopisLoad defaults to false, all existing behavior preserved

-----------------------------

Bug 29464 — Docházka: při úpravě záznamu chybí projekt

  Problém: Při editaci existujícího záznamu docházky přes tužku se v okně nezobrazoval přiřazený projekt, i když na záznamu byl uložený.

  Příčina: CiselnikButtonBox v XAML neměl binding na KodStavba. Data se při editaci správně načetla (konstruktor volal LoadStavby(inpt.KodStavba)),
  ale UI control o hodnotě nevěděl — chybělo propojení.

  Fix: Přidán Kod_Ciselnik="{Binding KodStavba, Mode=TwoWay}" na CiselnikButtonBox.

  FILE: Proconom\eConoM\Stavby\Detaily\DochazkaEditControl.xaml, line 206

  Rozsah změny: 1 řádek v XAML, žádná změna chování u nových záznamů ani jiných typů práce.

------------------x
  How the "Práva workflow" checkboxes work
--------------------

  Step 1 — Page loads: API fetches UserAdminInfoDTO

  When you open a user's admin page, it calls getUser(userId) which hits:

  GET /api/users/admin/{id} → GetUserAdminHandler.cs

  Returns UserAdminInfoDTO which contains all the checkbox values:
  workflowAdding: true
  workflowReporting: true
  workflowTemplateManaging: true
  ...

  This gets stored in local React state: userData.user

  ---
  Step 2 — Checkbox is ticked: only local state updates

  When you tick/untick a checkbox, nothing is sent to the API yet. It just updates userData in memory:

  // FILE: userDetailScene.tsx:879
  onChange={(e, checked) => {
      setUserData({
          user: {
              ...userData.user,
              workflowAdding: checked,   // ← just updates local state
          },
          ...
      });
      setChanged(true);  // ← enables the "Uložit" button
  }}

  ---
  Step 3 — "Uložit" button: sends everything to API

  When you click Uložit, handleSave() runs. It builds a UserAdminInfoDTO from the current local state and sends it to:

  PUT /api/users/admin → UpdateUserAdminHandler.cs

  // FILE: userDetailScene.tsx:138-169
  const user: UserAdminInfoDTO = {
      id: userData.user.id,
      workflowAdding: userData.user.workflowAdding,        // ← from checkbox
      workflowTemplateManaging: userData.user.workflowTemplateManaging,
      ...
  };
  await client.users_UpdateUserAdmin({ user });

  The API handler then writes straight to DB:
  // UpdateUserAdminHandler.cs:53
  requestedUser.CUzivateleOpravneni.PridaniWf = request.User.WorkflowAdding;
  requestedUser.CUzivateleOpravneni.SpravaSablonWf = request.User.WorkflowTemplateManaging;

  ---
  Full flow

  Page open
    ↓
  GET /api/users/admin/{id}  →  UserAdminInfoDTO
    ↓
  userData.user.workflowAdding = true  (shown in checkbox)

  User ticks/unticks checkbox
    ↓
  setUserData(...)  ← only local state, no API call

  User clicks "Uložit"
    ↓
  PUT /api/users/admin  ←  sends full UserAdminInfoDTO
    ↓
  UpdateUserAdminHandler writes to CUzivateleOpravneni table
    ↓
  Done

  ---

  1. DB column

  Default is 1 (true) — every new user gets this permission by default.

  FILE: EFModels\Models\CUzivateleOpravneni.cs:12
  public bool PridaniWf { get; set; }

  ---
  3. GET — loading the checkbox value

  FILE: TestApi\v1\Modules\Users\Handlers\GetUserAdminHandler.cs:114

  AutoMapper maps DB → DTO:
  .ForMember(d => d.WorkflowAdding, o => o.MapFrom(s => s.CUzivateleOpravneni.PridaniWf))
  Returns UserAdminInfoDTO.WorkflowAdding = true/false to the FE.

  4. FE — displays the checkbox

  FILE: src\admin\userDetailScene.tsx:877
  checked={userData.user.workflowAdding}

  ---
  5. PUT — saving after "Uložit"

  FILE: src\admin\userDetailScene.tsx:148
  workflowAdding: userData.user.workflowAdding   // packed into UserAdminInfoDTO
  Sent to PUT /api/users/admin → UpdateUserAdminHandler.cs

  ---
  6. Handler writes to DB

  FILE: TestApi\v1\Modules\Users\Handlers\UpdateUserAdminHandler.cs:53

  ---
  7. Auth — included in login response

  FILE: TestApi\Auth\Helpers\LoginResponseHelper.cs:58
  pridavaniWF: user.CUzivateleOpravneni.PridaniWf
  Goes into UzivatelAuthDTO → saved to localStorage → into UserContext.

  8. FE — gates the UI buttons

  FILE: src\workflow\components\workflowTemplates\workflowCard.tsx:173
  {user?.pridavaniWF && (
      <EditIcon />        // Aplikovat upravené
      <ContentCopyIcon /> // Znovu aplikovat
      <DeleteIcon />      // Smazat
  )}

  ---
  Complete chain visualized

  DB: c_uzivatele_opravneni.pridani_wf  (BIT, default 1)
    ↕  EF scaffold
  EFModel: CUzivateleOpravneni.PridaniWf  (bool)
    ↕  AutoMapper
  DTO: UserAdminInfoDTO.WorkflowAdding  (bool)
    ↕  GET /api/users/admin/{id}
  FE: userData.user.workflowAdding  → checkbox display
    ↕  PUT /api/users/admin  (on Uložit)
  Handler: PridaniWf = request.User.WorkflowAdding  → SaveChanges()
    ↕  login / token refresh
  Auth: UzivatelAuthDTO.PridavaniWF → localStorage → UserContext
    ↕  every component
  FE: user?.pridavaniWF → show/hide buttons

 Postman Test Tutorial — WF Action Rights
--------------------

  ---
  Prerequisites

  - API running on https://localhost:44301
  - SSL verification disabled in Postman (Settings → SSL certificate verification → OFF)

  ---
  Step 1 — Master auth

  POST https://localhost:44301/api/anonym/auth/authenticate/master

  - Authorization → No Auth
  - Body → raw → JSON:
  {
    "userName": "skrabal@proconom.cz",
    "password": "lopataCDE1*"
  }
  From response copy: authToken

  ---
  Step 2 — Get JWT token

  POST https://localhost:44301/api/Auth/databasetoken

  - Authorization → Bearer Token → paste authToken from Step 1
  - Body → raw → JSON:
  {
    "database": "Data_Develop"
  }
  From response copy: uzivatel.token — use this as Bearer Token in all steps below

  ---
  Step 3 — Template list has Aktivni field

  GET https://localhost:44301/api/workflow/template/all?ItemId=1&ItemType=2&CountType=0

  - Authorization → Bearer Token → uzivatel.token

  ✅ Each template has "aktivni": true/false
  ✅ Inactive templates are sorted to the bottom

  ---
  Step 4 — Deactivate a template

  Pick any id from Step 3 response.

  PUT https://localhost:44301/api/workflow/patterns/{id}/set-active

  - Authorization → Bearer Token → uzivatel.token
  - Body → raw → JSON:
  false
  ✅ Response: 200 {}

  Rerun Step 3 — that template now has "aktivni": false and is at the bottom.

  ---
  Step 5 — Reactivate the same template

  PUT https://localhost:44301/api/workflow/patterns/{id}/set-active

  - Same as Step 4 but body:
  true
  ✅ Response: 200 {}

  ---
  Step 6 — Admin user detail has new rights fields

  GET https://localhost:44301/api/users/user/5/admin

  - Authorization → Bearer Token → uzivatel.token

  ✅ Response contains "workflowAddingCopy" and "workflowAddingEdited"

  ---
  Step 7 — Save new rights

  PUT https://localhost:44301/api/users/user/admin

  - Authorization → Bearer Token → uzivatel.token
  - Body → raw → JSON:
  {
    "user": {
      "id": 5,

      "lastName": "Administrátor",

Goal

  Add checkbox "Nezahrnovat položky začínající na 0" to the Proměřené položky za období print widget. When checked, items (RPol) whose Znacka starts with
  "0" are excluded from the Excel report. The checkbox is mutually exclusive with the existing "Nezahrnovat Stavební díl se značkou 0" checkbox.

  ---
  Repo: Test_Nove_API

  1. TiskReportApi/Modules/Reports/Handlers/GetMereniPolozekZaObdobiExcelHandler.cs

  What changed: New property added to the MediatR request class.

  // BEFORE:
  public bool VyfiltrovatSDsNulouVeZnacce { get; set; } = false;

  // AFTER:
  public bool VyfiltrovatSDsNulouVeZnacce { get; set; } = false;
  public bool VyfiltrovatPolozkyZacinajiciNulou { get; set; } = false;  // NEW

  This property travels from the API endpoint → MediatR handler → report builder.

  ---
  2. TiskReportApi/Helpers/ReportMereniPolozekZaObdobiExcel.cs

  What changed: EF Core filtered .ThenInclude on RPol added in both include chains inside GetData().

  // Chain 1 (SSdPol path):
  .ThenInclude(x => x.RSd.Where(z => !(req.VyfiltrovatSDsNulouVeZnacce && z.Znacka.Equals("0"))))
      .ThenInclude(x => x.RPol.Where(p => !(req.VyfiltrovatPolozkyZacinajiciNulou && p.Znacka.StartsWith("0"))))  // NEW
          .ThenInclude(x => x.SSdPol.Where(y => y.DatumMereni >= _od && y.DatumMereni <= _do))

  // Chain 2 (KMjNavigation path):
  .ThenInclude(x => x.RSd.Where(z => !(req.VyfiltrovatSDsNulouVeZnacce && z.Znacka.Equals("0"))))
      .ThenInclude(x => x.RPol.Where(p => !(req.VyfiltrovatPolozkyZacinajiciNulou && p.Znacka.StartsWith("0"))))  // NEW
          .ThenInclude(x => x.KMjNavigation)

  Both chains had to be updated — EF Core loads each chain independently and merges results into the entity graph. Filtering only one would let the
  unfiltered chain repopulate the RPol collection.

  No database changes. The filter is purely in-memory at the EF query level.

  ---
  Repo: Proconom (WPF)

  3. eConoM/Servers/ReportServer/IReportServer.cs

  What changed: New filtrovatPolNula parameter added to the interface method.

  // BEFORE:
  Task<FileResponse> GetPromerenostZaObdobi(..., bool filtrovatSDsNulou, CancellationToken cancellationToken);

  // AFTER:
  Task<FileResponse> GetPromerenostZaObdobi(..., bool filtrovatSDsNulou, bool filtrovatPolNula, CancellationToken cancellationToken);

  ---
  4. eConoM/Servers/ReportServer/ReportServer.cs

  What changed: Implementation signature updated to match the interface. The parameter is accepted but not yet forwarded to the NSwag client call — pending
  NSwag regeneration.

  public async Task<FileResponse> GetPromerenostZaObdobi(
      int stavbaId, List<int> objektyIds, DateTime? _od, DateTime? _do,
      bool filtrovatSDsNulou, bool filtrovatPolNula,   // NEW param

--------------- !!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!! HIERARCHY !!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!§
 ---
  What each entity actually is

  ┌────────┬──────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │ Entity │     Name     │                                         What it is                                          │
  ├────────┼──────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ RSd    │ Stavební díl │ The section (SD) — a grouping, not a položka                                                │
  ├────────┼──────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ RPol   │ Položka      │ The actual budget line item under an SD — this is what you call "pol"                       │
  ├────────┼──────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ SSdPol │ Měření       │ A single measurement event for one RPol — records quantity measured on a specific date      │
  ├────────┼──────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ ZbvPol │ ZBV položka  │ A line item within a change order — defines the allowed quantity for an RPol under that ZBV │
  └────────┴──────────────┴─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  ---
  Hierarchy

  SStavby (stavba)
    └── RObjekty (stavební objekt)
          └── RSd (stavební díl — the section)
                └── RPol (položka — budget line item)
                      └── SSdPol (měření — one measurement record, date + quantity)
                            └── KZbvNavigation (ZBV — change order header)
                                  └── ZbvPol (položka ZBV — allowed quantity for RPol under this ZBV)

  ---
  Key distinction inside SSdPol

  A measurement (SSdPol) can be either:
  - SOD measurement — KZbv == null — measures progress against the original contract
  - ZBV measurement — KZbv != null — measures progress against a specific change order

  This is why SetPol splits them:
  // SOD measurements only
  pol.SSdPol.Where(x => x.KZbv == null)

  // ZBV measurements only — handled separately in SetSSDPolZeZBV
  pol.SSdPol.Where(x => x.KZbvNavigation != null && !x.KZbvNavigation.Stav)

  So SSdPol is not a type of položka — it is a measurement event. RPol and ZbvPol are the two types of položky.

  ---

!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!! Final Complete Proposal !!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!

  Data hierarchy being loaded

  SStavby (stavba)
    └── RObjekty (objekty/SO)
          └── RSd  ← NO LONGER FILTERED HERE
                └── RPol (položky)  ← FILTER APPLIED HERE
                      ├── KMjNavigation (unit of measure)
                      └── SSdPol (individual measurements)  ← DATE FILTER HERE
                            └── KZbvNavigation (ZBV)
                                  └── ZbvPol

  ---
  What changes and why

  RSd.Where(...) removed from both include paths.
  Reason: filtering no longer happens at RSd level. All stavební díly are loaded regardless of their Znacka.

  In-memory RPol filter added after the null check.
  Reason: one flag is always active. The ternary picks the correct predicate — StartsWith or Contains — based on which flag is true. Items that match are removed from
  sd.RPol. If all items under an RSd are removed, the SD disappears naturally via the existing x.RPol.Any() guard in SetSD (line 393) — no extra code needed there.

  Date filter on SSdPol unchanged — still DB-side on the second include path.

  ---
  FILE: TiskReportApi/Helpers/ReportMereniPolozekZaObdobiExcel.cs
  METHOD: GetData()

  protected override async Task GetData()
  {
      SStavby stav = await _ctx.SStavby
          .AsNoTracking()
          .AsSplitQuery()
          .Where(s => s.XId == req.StavbaId)

          .Include(s => s.SStavbyNastaveni)

          // First path — loads KMjNavigation (unit of measure)
          .Include(s => s.RObjekty)
              .ThenInclude(o => o.RSd)
                  .ThenInclude(sd => sd.RPol)
                      .ThenInclude(p => p.KMjNavigation)

          // Second path — loads measurements filtered by date + ZBV
          .Include(s => s.RObjekty)
              .ThenInclude(o => o.RSd)
                  .ThenInclude(sd => sd.RPol)
                      .ThenInclude(p => p.SSdPol
                          .Where(m =>
                              m.DatumMereni >= _od &&
                              m.DatumMereni <= _do))
                          .ThenInclude(sp => sp.KZbvNavigation)
                              .ThenInclude(z => z.ZbvPol)

          .FirstOrDefaultAsync();

      if (stav == null)
          throw new BadDataException("Stavba nenalezena");

      // Filter individual RPol items — one flag is always true
      // VyfiltrovatPolozkyZacinajiciNulou → exclude items whose Znacka starts with "0"
      // VyfiltrovatSDsNulouVeZnacce       → exclude items whose Znacka contains "0"
      // RSd with no surviving items is excluded from output by SetSD's RPol.Any() check
      foreach (var objekt in stav.RObjekty)
          foreach (var sd in objekt.RSd)
              sd.RPol = sd.RPol
                  .Where(p => req.VyfiltrovatPolozkyZacinajiciNulou
                      ? !p.Znacka.StartsWith("0")
                      : !p.Znacka.Contains("0"))
                  .ToList();

      data = new ReportMereniPolozekZaObdobiExcelData
      {
          Stavba = stav
      };
  }

  ---
  After GetData() completes

  - data.Stavba.RObjekty — all objekty of the stavba
  - Each objekt.RSd — all SDs, no Znacka filter at this level
  - Each sd.RPol — only položky that passed the RPol filter (Znacka does not start with "0" or does not contain "0", depending on which flag is active)
  - Each pol.KMjNavigation — measurement unit (always loaded)
  - Each pol.SSdPol — only measurements within the date range (or empty list if none)
  - Each SSdPol.KZbvNavigation.ZbvPol — ZBV data for those measurements

Revised Proposal — Schválené ZBV v informacích stavby

  Architecture summary

  The setting travels: DB column → EF model → WCF Service1.cs (read) / UpdateUserSettingsHandler (write) → UzivatelNastaveniDC → NSwag → WPF UzivatelNastaveniDTO. The GetCenyStavby
  endpoint is already in ManagerStavebController.cs (the non-Desktop controller), so adding schvaleneOnly as a query param automatically satisfies the web variant requirement — no second
  controller needed.

  ---
  PREREQUISITE — DB column (Karel adds manually)

  ALTER TABLE Data_Master.dbo.c_uzivatele_nastaveni
  ADD zbv_info_schvalene BIT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;

  ---
  PHASE 1 — API (implement now)

  AFTER: line 32 (public bool? ZbvSouhrne { get; set; })

  public bool ZahrnoutPouzeSchvaleneZBV { get; set; } = false;

  AFTER: line 49 (uzivNastaveni.ZbvSouhrne = req.Nastaveni.ZBVPoObjektech;)

  Step 4 — WCF read-back: map DB row → DTO (two places)

  FILE: Test_Nove_API/CustomUsernamePasswordAuth.Service/Service1.cs

  CHANGED — occurrence #1 (line 3214 block — the if branch with full user settings)
  AFTER: line 3214 (ZBVPoObjektech = ...)

  ZahrnoutPouzeSchvaleneZBV = x.Field<bool?>("zbv_info_schvalene") != null ? x.Field<bool?>("zbv_info_schvalene").Value : false,

  CHANGED — occurrence #2 (line 3266 block — the else branch)
  AFTER: line 3266 (ZBVPoObjektech = ...)

  ZahrnoutPouzeSchvaleneZBV = x.Field<bool?>("zbv_info_schvalene") != null ? x.Field<bool?>("zbv_info_schvalene").Value : false,

  Why both: The if branch reads the full row (user has a personal settings record); the else branch reads a default/fallback row. Both must return the setting or the WPF will always see
  false after a cold login.

  ---
  Step 5 — GetRPols: add ThenIncludes for KZbvNavigation

  FILE: Test_Nove_API/EFModels/CustomModels/SStavby.cs
  METHOD: SStavbyPrice.GetRPols() (around line 553)

  CURRENT:
  List<RPol> pols = ctx.RPol
      .Include(x => x.ZbvPol)
      .Include(x => x.SSdPol)
      .Include(x => x.PPol)
          .ThenInclude(x => x.KZpNavigation.FFakturyPol)
      .Where(x => x.KStavba == Entity.XId)
      .AsSplitQuery()
      .AsNoTracking()
      .ToList();

  REPLACE WITH:
  List<RPol> pols = ctx.RPol
      .Include(x => x.ZbvPol)
          .ThenInclude(z => z.KZbvNavigation)
      .Include(x => x.SSdPol)
          .ThenInclude(z => z.KZbvNavigation)
      .Include(x => x.PPol)
          .ThenInclude(x => x.KZpNavigation.FFakturyPol)
      .Include(x => x.PPol)
          .ThenInclude(x => x.KZpNavigation.KZbvNavigation)
      .Where(x => x.KStavba == Entity.XId)
      .AsSplitQuery()
      .AsNoTracking()
      .ToList();

  Why 3 ThenIncludes: Three distinct navigation chains need KZbvNavigation.Stav:
  - ZbvPol → KZbvNavigation — for GetPricesZbv()
  - SSdPol → KZbvNavigation — for GetPricesZbvMereni()
  - PPol → KZpNavigation → KZbvNavigation — for GetPricesZbvZp() and GetPricesZbvFaktury()

  The PPol Include must be repeated as a new chain because ThenInclude from FFakturyPol can't branch — EF Core requires a new Include(x => x.PPol) root to reach a sibling of FFakturyPol.

  INTENTIONALLY UNCHANGED — all other callers of GetRPols():
  GetRPols() is an abstract method overridden only in SStavbyPrice. No other subclass is affected.

  Step 6 — PriceEntity: add schvaleneOnly filter to ZBV methods

  FILE: Test_Nove_API/EFModels/Interfaces/PriceEntity.cs

  Replace the following methods (full replacements shown):

  GetPrices() — add parameter, propagate to all ZBV and Actual calls:

  public virtual CenyStavbaDTO GetPrices(bool schvaleneOnly = false)
  {
      CenyStavbaDTO item = new();
      item.Aktualni = GetPricesActual(schvaleneOnly);
      item.AktualniNamereno = GetPricesActualMereni(schvaleneOnly);
      item.AktualniCerpano = GetPricesActualZp(schvaleneOnly: schvaleneOnly);
      item.AktualniFakturovano = GetPricesActualFaktury(schvaleneOnly);

      item.Sod = GetPricesSoD();
      item.SodNamereno = GetPricesSoDMereni();
      item.SodCerpano = GetPricesSoDZp();
      item.SodFakturovano = GetPricesSoDFaktury();

      item.Zbv = GetPricesZbv(schvaleneOnly);
      item.ZbvNamereno = GetPricesZbvMereni(schvaleneOnly);
      item.ZbvCerpano = GetPricesZbvZp(schvaleneOnly: schvaleneOnly);
      item.ZbvFakturovano = GetPricesZbvFaktury(schvaleneOnly);

      return item;
  }

  GetPricesZbv() — approval filter on ZbvPol:

  public CenyDTO GetPricesZbv(bool schvaleneOnly = false)
  {
      List<PriceTax> prices = GetPriceForEveryTax(x =>
      {
          IEnumerable<ZbvPol> zbvPols = x.ZbvPol;
          if (schvaleneOnly)
              zbvPols = zbvPols.Where(z => z.KZbvNavigation.Stav == false);  //stav 0/false == schvalene
          return zbvPols.Sum(z => z.GetRoundedPrice(z.MnozstviZbv, AmountDecimalPlaces.Sod, AmountDecimalPlaces.Joc));
      });
      return PriceTax.ToCenyDTO(prices);
  }

  GetPricesZbvMereni() — approval filter on SSdPol.KZbvNavigation.Stav:

  public CenyDTO GetPricesZbvMereni(bool schvaleneOnly = false)
  {
      List<PriceTax> prices = GetPriceForEveryTax(x => x.SSdPol
          .Where(s => s.KZbv is not null && (!schvaleneOnly || s.KZbvNavigation.Stav == false))  //stav 0/false == schvalene
          .Sum(z => z.GetRoundedPrice(z.Mnozstvi ?? 0, AmountDecimalPlaces.Zp, AmountDecimalPlaces.Joc)));
      return PriceTax.ToCenyDTO(prices);
  }

  GetPricesZbvZp() — approval filter on PPol.KZpNavigation.KZbvNavigation.Stav:

  public CenyDTO GetPricesZbvZp(bool schvaleneOnly = false)
  {
      List<PriceTax> prices = GetPriceForEveryTax(x => x.PPol
          .Where(y => y.KZpNavigation.KZbv is not null && (!schvaleneOnly || y.KZpNavigation.KZbvNavigation.Stav == false))  //stav 0/false == schvalene
          .Sum(z => z.GetRoundedPrice(z.Mnozstvi, AmountDecimalPlaces.Zp, AmountDecimalPlaces.Joc)));
      return PriceTax.ToCenyDTO(prices);
  }

  The overload GetPricesZbvZp(DateTime datumOd, DateTime datumDo) is NOT changed — it is used only by GetPricesActualZp(datumOd, datumDo) which is not called from GetPrices().

  GetPricesZbvFaktury() — approval filter on PPol.KZpNavigation.KZbvNavigation.Stav:

  public CenyDTO GetPricesZbvFaktury(bool schvaleneOnly = false)
  {
      List<PriceTax> prices = GetPriceForEveryTax(y => y.PPol
          .Where(x => x.KZpNavigation.KZbv is not null && x.KZpNavigation.FFakturyPol.Any() && (!schvaleneOnly || x.KZpNavigation.KZbvNavigation.Stav == false))  //stav 0/false ==
  schvalene
          .Sum(z => z.GetRoundedPrice(z.Mnozstvi, AmountDecimalPlaces.Zp, AmountDecimalPlaces.Joc)));
      return PriceTax.ToCenyDTO(prices);
  }

  GetPricesActual(), GetPricesActualMereni(), GetPricesActualFaktury() — propagate:

  public CenyDTO GetPricesActual(bool schvaleneOnly = false)
  {
      CenyDTO SoD = GetPricesSoD();
      CenyDTO Zbv = GetPricesZbv(schvaleneOnly);
      return SoD.SumCeny(Zbv);
  }

  public CenyDTO GetPricesActualMereni(bool schvaleneOnly = false)
  {
      CenyDTO SoD = GetPricesSoDMereni();
      CenyDTO Zbv = GetPricesZbvMereni(schvaleneOnly);
      return SoD.SumCeny(Zbv);
  }

  public CenyDTO GetPricesActualFaktury(bool schvaleneOnly = false)
  {
      CenyDTO SoD = GetPricesSoDFaktury();
      CenyDTO Zbv = GetPricesZbvFaktury(schvaleneOnly);
      return SoD.SumCeny(Zbv);
  }

  GetPricesActualZp() — propagate (add schvaleneOnly after existing optional params):

  public CenyDTO GetPricesActualZp(DateTime? datumOd = null, DateTime? datumDo = null, bool schvaleneOnly = false)
  {
      if (!datumOd.HasValue)
          datumOd = DateTime.UnixEpoch;
      if (!datumDo.HasValue)
          datumDo = DateTime.MaxValue;

      CenyDTO SoD = GetPricesSoDZp(datumOd.Value, datumDo.Value);
      CenyDTO Zbv = GetPricesZbvZp(datumOd.Value, datumDo.Value);
      return SoD.SumCeny(Zbv);
  }

  Note: GetPricesActualZp() calls the datumOd/datumDo overload of GetPricesZbvZp() (not the schvaleneOnly overload). This is correct — the date-range variant is used for financial plan
  calculations, not for the stavba info dashboard. The schvaleneOnly parameter on GetPricesActualZp() has no effect in the current implementation but is added for future consistency. If
  you prefer to skip it and pass schvaleneOnly = false explicitly from GetPrices(), that is equally valid.

  INTENTIONALLY UNCHANGED — GetPricesZbvZp(DateTime datumOd, DateTime datumDo) overload:
  Used by VratRekapitulaciObjektuHandler (reports) which has no schvaleneOnly concept.

  INTENTIONALLY UNCHANGED — all other callers of GetPrices(), GetPricesZbv(), GetPricesActual():
  UspVratDataSoupisPraciProcedure.cs, VratRekapitulaciObjektuHandler.cs, GetZPStavbaReportHandler.cs, GetCenyStavebnihoDiluHandler.cs, GetCenyObjektuHandler.cs all call with no arguments →
   schvaleneOnly = false default → zero behavior change.

  }

  TypeScript client). No separate controller or route is needed. The optional ?schvaleneOnly=true query param is backward-compatible for both clients.

     protected override async Task GetData()
     {
         //A solution
         SStavby stav = await _ctx.SStavby
             .AsNoTracking()
             .AsSplitQuery()
             .Where(s => s.XId == req.StavbaId)

             .Include(s => s.SStavbyNastaveni)

             // -------- First include path --------
             .Include(s => s.RObjekty)
                 .ThenInclude(o => o.RSd.Where(sd =>
                     // INLINE filtering logic ✅
                     (!req.VyfiltrovatPolozkyZacinajiciNulou || !sd.Znacka.StartsWith("0")) &&
                     (!req.VyfiltrovatSDsNulouVeZnacce || sd.Znacka != "0")
                 ))
                     .ThenInclude(sd => sd.RPol)
                         .ThenInclude(p => p.KMjNavigation)

             // -------- Second include path --------
             .Include(s => s.RObjekty)
                 .ThenInclude(o => o.RSd.Where(sd =>
                     // SAME INLINE filtering logic ✅
                     (!req.VyfiltrovatPolozkyZacinajiciNulou || !sd.Znacka.StartsWith("0")) &&
                     (!req.VyfiltrovatSDsNulouVeZnacce || sd.Znacka != "0")
                 ))
                     .ThenInclude(sd => sd.RPol)
                         .ThenInclude(p => p.SSdPol
                             .Where(m =>
                                 m.DatumMereni >= _od &&
                                 m.DatumMereni <= _do))
                             .ThenInclude(sp => sp.KZbvNavigation)
                                 .ThenInclude(z => z.ZbvPol)

             .FirstOrDefaultAsync();

         if (stav == null)
             throw new BadDataException("Stavba nenalezena");

         data = new ReportMereniPolozekZaObdobiExcelData
         {
             Stavba = stav
         };
     }

https://tfs-server.proconom.local/ProconomSW/PROCONOM/_workitems/edit/28936


Root cause

  IKeyedServiceProvider is not registered in the .NET DI container — only IServiceProvider is auto-registered. The change in the PR commit replaced IServiceProvider with IKeyedServiceProvider, which breaks DI
   validation.

  The method GetRequiredKeyedService<T>(key) is an extension method on IServiceProvider (from ServiceProviderKeyedServiceExtensions), so IKeyedServiceProvider is not needed at all.

 Both GetHromadnyZaznamMereniDataHandlerWeb and GetDataProVymeruProvedenychPraciHandlerWeb have IKeyedServiceProvider in their constructor — change both to IServiceProvider. That's the entire fix.

Simple explanation

  The DI container is like a registry of services

  When the app starts, .NET registers all services into a container — a dictionary of "here's what I can provide when someone asks."

  IServiceProvider is automatically registered — it's the container itself. Any class can ask for it in its constructor and .NET will hand it over.

  IKeyedServiceProvider is a sub-interface of IServiceProvider (adds support for named/keyed lookups). But .NET does not register it separately. If a class asks for it in its constructor, .NET looks in the
  registry, finds nothing, and throws.

  ---
  The method confusion

  GetRequiredKeyedService<T>(key) — this is just an extension method sitting on top of IServiceProvider. Think of it like a helper function:

  // Defined as extension on IServiceProvider:
  public static T GetRequiredKeyedService<T>(this IServiceProvider sp, object key) { ... }

  So you call it on IServiceProvider — you never need IKeyedServiceProvider directly.

  ---
  What went wrong

  Someone thought: "I'm using keyed services, so I should inject the keyed version of the provider."

  That logic sounds reasonable but is wrong. The keyed lookup capability comes from the extension method, not from the type you inject. IServiceProvider is enough.

  Analogy: You don't need a "sports car driver's license" to press the sport mode button on a regular car. The regular license + the button is all you need.



------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


https://tfs-server.proconom.local/ProconomSW/PROCONOM/_workitems/edit/28653

The VideoHelpClient source is not in your local source/repos directory — it's a compiled DLL only (bin/Debug/VideoHelpClient.dll). The phone number is hardcoded inside that external library's source, which
  lives elsewhere (likely a separate repository not checked out here).

  The window "VIDEO NÁPOVĚDA" with the phone number is entirely owned by VideoHelpClient.VideoHelpWindow. To remove the phone number you need to find and edit that project's source — it's not in the Proconom
  repo.

  Do you know where the VideoHelpClient source repository is located?


<Button x:Name="btnHelp"


Why your manual copy doesn't work

  Proconom references VideoHelpClient as a NuGet package (version 2.0.399), not as a file reference:

  <!-- Proconom/eConoM/PROCONOM.csproj -->
  <PackageReference Include="VideoHelpClient" Version="2.0.399" />

  Every time you build, MSBuild resolves the DLL from the NuGet package cache:
  C:\Users\karel.skrabal\.nuget\packages\videohelpclient\2.0.399\lib\net471\VideoHelpClient.dll

  It then copies that cached DLL into bin\Debug\. So your manually placed DLL in bin\Debug\ gets overwritten on every build by the cached package version.

  ---
  The right procedure

  Option A — For local testing only (quick)

  Copy your freshly built DLL directly into the NuGet cache, replacing the cached version:

  C:\Users\karel.skrabal\.nuget\packages\videohelpclient\2.0.399\lib\net471\VideoHelpClient.dll
  C:\Users\karel.skrabal\.nuget\packages\videohelpclientbase\2.0.399\lib\net471\VideoHelpClientBase.dll

  NuGet will not re-download if the version is already present in the cache. Build Proconom → MSBuild picks up your modified DLL from cache → copies it to bin\Debug\.

  Option B — Proper/permanent flow

  1. Make changes in Proconom_Desktop_2
  2. Build Proconom_Desktop_2 (Release)
  3. Pack a new NuGet package (bump version, e.g. 2.0.400)
  4. Push to the internal Proconom feed (http://192.168.4.19/...)
  5. Update version in PROCONOM.csproj and OSharedControlsWPF.csproj → NuGet restore

  ---
  For your immediate need: Option A is fastest. Build Proconom_Desktop_2, then copy the output DLLs to the NuGet cache paths above, then rebuild Proconom.


For Release build it would be:
  VideoHelpClient\bin\Release\net471\VideoHelpClient.dll
  VideoHelpClientBase\bin\Release\net471\VideoHelpClientBase.dll

  ---
  Full procedure (Option A — quick local test)

  1. Build Proconom_Desktop_2 in VS (Debug or Release)
  2. Copy the two net471 DLLs to the NuGet cache (replacing the old version):
  C:\Users\karel.skrabal\.nuget\packages\videohelpclient\2.0.399\lib\net471\VideoHelpClient.dll
  C:\Users\karel.skrabal\.nuget\packages\videohelpclientbase\2.0.399\lib\net471\VideoHelpClientBase.dll
  3. Rebuild Proconom — MSBuild picks from cache → copies to bin\Debug\ → your change is live

  The bin\Debug\ and bin\Release\ paths in Proconom are just MSBuild copy destinations, never the source of truth.

Kde se všude dá odstranit soubor ze složky!!!!!!!!!!!!!!


Níže máš slíbený **TAHÁK** pro **variantu 1 = fyzicky smazat soubor ze složky v DMS** (tj. odstranit soubor z úložiště), a to včetně „cest“ z modulů **ZBV / ESD / Rozpočet / Měření**. [\[fr-prod.as...rosoft.com\]](https://fr-prod.asyncgw.teams.microsoft.com/v1/objects/0-frca-d15-21a22fbf97a276ae6484e4cbfa711f28/views/original/crop_docs.png)

***

# 🧾 TAHÁK: Kde a jak smazat dokument **ze složky (DMS)**

## Základní pravidlo (pro „fyzické smazání“)

✅ **Fyzické mazání souboru ze složky** se dělá v **Dokumenty → DMS** (nebo v dialogu, který z jiného modulu otevře přímo DMS). Modul Dokumenty (DMS) má mezi standardními akcemi nad souborem i **odstranění**. [\[fr-prod.as...rosoft.com\]](https://fr-prod.asyncgw.teams.microsoft.com/v1/objects/0-frca-d15-21a22fbf97a276ae6484e4cbfa711f28/views/original/crop_docs.png)

> **Pozor na záložku „Dokumenty prvku“**: ta slouží k archivaci/vazbám na prvek; pro „smazání ze složky“ vždy přepni na **DMS**. [\[fr-prod.as...rosoft.com\]](https://fr-prod.asyncgw.teams.microsoft.com/v1/objects/0-frca-d15-21a22fbf97a276ae6484e4cbfa711f28/views/original/crop_docs.png)

***

## 1) 📁 Univerzální cesta (funguje vždy): **Dokumenty → DMS**

*(nejjistější a obecná cesta pro smazání libovolného souboru ze složky)* [\[fr-prod.as...rosoft.com\]](https://fr-prod.asyncgw.teams.microsoft.com/v1/objects/0-frca-d15-21a22fbf97a276ae6484e4cbfa711f28/views/original/crop_docs.png)

**Klikací cesta:**

1.  **Manažer staveb** → vyber **stavbu**
2.  Otevři modul **Dokumenty**
3.  Přepni na záložku **DMS** (strom složek)
4.  Najdi složku → označ soubor
5.  Smaž soubor jedním z typických způsobů:
    *   **Tlačítko „Odstranit“** v horní liště (aktivuje se po výběru souboru) [\[fr-prod.as...rosoft.com\]](https://fr-prod.asyncgw.teams.microsoft.com/v1/objects/0-frca-d15-21a22fbf97a276ae6484e4cbfa711f28/views/original/crop_docs.png)
    *   nebo **pravé tlačítko na soubor (kontextové menu)** → „Odstranit“ (obecná logika UI, manuál pracuje s kontextovými menu napříč aplikací) [\[fr-prod.as...rosoft.com\]](https://fr-prod.asyncgw.teams.microsoft.com/v1/objects/0-frca-d15-21a22fbf97a276ae6484e4cbfa711f28/views/original/crop_docs.png)

***

## 2) 🧾 ZBV (Změny): **ZBV → Dokumenty (dialog) → DMS → Odstranit**

Na tvém screenshotu je vidět, že v modulu **Změny (ZBV)** lze otevřít okno **Dokumenty** a přepnout na **DMS** – a je tam i tlačítko **„Odstranit“** (mazání). *(dle screenshotu)*

**Klikací cesta (prakticky):**

1.  Otevři **Změny (ZBV)** → vyber konkrétní ZBV
2.  Otevři okno **Dokumenty** (tak jak to máš na screenshotu)
3.  V okně Dokumenty přepni na **DMS**
4.  Vyber soubor ve složce
5.  Klikni **„Odstranit“** (nebo kontextové menu na souboru → odstranit)

> Manuál potvrzuje existenci práce s dokumenty a DMS operací v modulu Dokumenty (včetně odstranění) a že ZBV pracuje s dokumenty (např. nahrávání PDF do ZBV detailu). [\[fr-prod.as...rosoft.com\]](https://fr-prod.asyncgw.teams.microsoft.com/v1/objects/0-frca-d15-21a22fbf97a276ae6484e4cbfa711f28/views/original/crop_docs.png)

***

## 3) 🏗️ ESD (Stavební deník): **ESD → Správa dokumentů (ikona složky) → DMS → Odstranit**

Manuál přímo uvádí, že v ESD je vstup do **správy dokumentů** přes **ikonu složky**. [\[fr-prod.as...rosoft.com\]](https://fr-prod.asyncgw.teams.microsoft.com/v1/objects/0-frca-d15-21a22fbf97a276ae6484e4cbfa711f28/views/original/crop_docs.png)

**Klikací cesta:**

1.  **Manažer staveb** → stavba → otevři **Stavební deník (ESD)**
2.  Klikni na **ikonu složky** = **Správa dokumentů** [\[fr-prod.as...rosoft.com\]](https://fr-prod.asyncgw.teams.microsoft.com/v1/objects/0-frca-d15-21a22fbf97a276ae6484e4cbfa711f28/views/original/crop_docs.png)
3.  Pokud se otevře stejné/analogické okno Dokumenty:
    *   přepni na **DMS**
    *   vyber soubor
    *   **Odstranit**
4.  Když se otevře jen seznam bez „Odstranit“:
    *   použij univerzální cestu **Dokumenty → DMS** a smaž soubor tam [\[fr-prod.as...rosoft.com\]](https://fr-prod.asyncgw.teams.microsoft.com/v1/objects/0-frca-d15-21a22fbf97a276ae6484e4cbfa711f28/views/original/crop_docs.png)

***

## 4) 📏 Měření: **Měření → (zjisti název přílohy) → Dokumenty → DMS → Odstranit**

Manuál uvádí, že v modulu **Měření** se pracuje s fotodokumentací a lze nahrávat PDF/JPEG dokumenty.   
Ale pro **fyzické smazání ze složky** je opět jistota **DMS**. [\[fr-prod.as...rosoft.com\]](https://fr-prod.asyncgw.teams.microsoft.com/v1/objects/0-frca-d15-21a22fbf97a276ae6484e4cbfa711f28/views/original/crop_docs.png)

**Klikací cesta:**

1.  **Měření** → otevři položku/záznam → zjisti **název souboru** (příloha)
2.  Přejdi do **Dokumenty → DMS** [\[fr-prod.as...rosoft.com\]](https://fr-prod.asyncgw.teams.microsoft.com/v1/objects/0-frca-d15-21a22fbf97a276ae6484e4cbfa711f28/views/original/crop_docs.png)
3.  Najdi soubor (buď ve správné složce, nebo přes vyhledávání)
4.  **Odstranit**

***

## 5) 💰 Rozpočet: **Rozpočet → (kontext stavby) → Dokumenty → DMS → Odstranit**

Rozpočet modul v manuálu popisuje hlavně strukturu rozpočtu a položky; pro práci se soubory ve složkách je opět „obecné“ místo **Dokumenty/DMS**. [\[fr-prod.as...rosoft.com\]](https://fr-prod.asyncgw.teams.microsoft.com/v1/objects/0-frca-d15-21a22fbf97a276ae6484e4cbfa711f28/views/original/crop_docs.png)

**Klikací cesta:**

1.  **Rozpočet** (na dané stavbě) → když potřebuješ smazat dokument ze složky…
2.  …jdi do **Dokumenty → DMS**
3.  Najdi soubor → **Odstranit** [\[fr-prod.as...rosoft.com\]](https://fr-prod.asyncgw.teams.microsoft.com/v1/objects/0-frca-d15-21a22fbf97a276ae6484e4cbfa711f28/views/original/crop_docs.png)

***

# ✅ Rychlé shrnutí „kde fyzicky mažu soubor ze složky“

*   **Dokumenty → DMS** (hlavní univerzální místo pro mazání souborů ze složek) [\[fr-prod.as...rosoft.com\]](https://fr-prod.asyncgw.teams.microsoft.com/v1/objects/0-frca-d15-21a22fbf97a276ae6484e4cbfa711f28/views/original/crop_docs.png)
*   **ZBV → Dokumenty (dialog) → DMS → Odstranit** *(potvrzeno tvým screenshotem)*
*   **ESD → Správa dokumentů (ikona složky) → DMS → Odstranit** (vstup do správy dokumentů je v manuálu) [\[fr-prod.as...rosoft.com\]](https://fr-prod.asyncgw.teams.microsoft.com/v1/objects/0-frca-d15-21a22fbf97a276ae6484e4cbfa711f28/views/original/crop_docs.png)
*   **Měření / Rozpočet**: fyzické mazání řeš přes **Dokumenty → DMS** [\[fr-prod.as...rosoft.com\]](https://fr-prod.asyncgw.teams.microsoft.com/v1/objects/0-frca-d15-21a22fbf97a276ae6484e4cbfa711f28/views/original/crop_docs.png)

***

## 🔐 Poznámka k oprávněním (typický důvod, proč „Odstranit“ nevidíš)

Manuál zmiňuje, že viditelnost/akce nad dokumenty mohou být řízené právy administrátorem. Pokud tedy **„Odstranit“** chybí nebo je šedé, bývá to často **právy**. [\[fr-prod.as...rosoft.com\]](https://fr-prod.asyncgw.teams.microsoft.com/v1/objects/0-frca-d15-21a22fbf97a276ae6484e4cbfa711f28/views/original/crop_docs.png)

  ---
  DETAILED FINDINGS

  FINDING 1 (HIGH) — No SeedBuilder usage in any file

  All 8 files write directly to DB via db.XYZ.Add() + SaveChangesAsync(). This violates rules 4.1 and 4.2 which state:

  "Zápis do databáze se provádí vždy přes SeedBuilder"
  "Veškeré zápisy do databáze provádí pouze SeedBuilder"

  Affected files: All 8 test files.

  Impact: If existing SeedBuilder partial methods for SUtvary, SStavby etc. already handle required field defaults, the manual
  seeding duplicates that logic. If any new entity tables are needed (e.g., ZbvZbv, ZbvPol, RPol, RSd, RObjekty, CPozice,
  SStavbyNastaveni), new {TableName}Seed.cs files with both overloads should be created.

  Note: Existing SeedBuilder has methods for SUtvary, SStavby, CPartneri, CSdruzeni, FFaktury, SStavbySdruzeni, SStavDenik. Missing
   from SeedBuilder: RObjekty, RSd, RPol, ZbvZbv, ZbvPol, SStavbyNastaveni, CPozice. These would need to be added as new partial
  class SeedBuilder files.

  ---
  FINDING 2 (HIGH) — No 401 (Unauthorized) tests

  Per rule 3.3, every test file should cover authorization: "Autorizace — neautentizovaný přístup → 401".

  None of the 8 files tests what happens when an unauthenticated client calls the endpoint. The expected pattern is:

  [Fact]
  public async Task GetXxx_Unauthenticated_Returns401()
  {
      // Arrange
      await _fixture.ResetAndSeedAsync();

      // Act & Assert
      TiskClient anonymousClient = new(_fixture.CreateClient());
      ApiException ex = await Assert.ThrowsAsync<ApiException>(() =>
          anonymousClient.Reports_GetXxxAsync(...));
      Assert.Equal((int)HttpStatusCode.Unauthorized, ex.StatusCode);
  }

  Affected: All 8 files — 8 missing 401 tests (16 if desktop + web each need one).

  ---
  FINDING 3 (MEDIUM) — Assert.ThrowsAnyAsync<Exception> instead of specific status code

  Files affected:
  - GetNabidkovySoupisTest.cs:55 — ThrowsAnyAsync<Exception> with comment "handler nemá null-check"
  - GetZaverecneVyporadaniDataTest.cs:113,126 — ThrowsAnyAsync<Exception> for missing CPozice edge case

  Per rule 5.2: "Vždy ověřit ex.StatusCode — nestačí jen to, že výjimka byla vyhozena."

  Using ThrowsAnyAsync<Exception> means the test passes for any exception (NullRef, timeout, network error, etc.), which makes it
  fragile and non-specific. If the handler truly has no null-check, this is a handler bug that should be reported, and the test
  should ideally assert a concrete expected behavior (e.g., 400 or 500).

  The comments explain why (ThrowsAnyAsync is used because the handler lacks a null-check), which is useful documentation, but the
  tests still technically violate rule 5.2.

  ---
  FINDING 4 (MEDIUM) — Missing // Act / // Assert comment separators

  File: GetSoupisPraciDataTest.cs
  - GetKomplexniSoupisPraciData_ValidStavba_Web_Returns200 (lines 76-89) — missing // Act and // Assert separators; code flows
  directly from Arrange into client call and assertions without section markers.

  Per rule 3.1: "Každý test musí být rozdělen do sekcí oddělených komentáři."

  ---
  FINDING 5 (LOW) — Massive seed data duplication

  All 8 files contain nearly identical SeedStavbaAsync() methods that create the same structure: SUtvary → SStavby → RObjekty → RSd
   → RPol × 2 → SStavbyNastaveni. This ~50-line block is copy-pasted across all files with only minor variations (Znacka, Nazev).

  This is a code smell but not a rule violation per se. If SeedBuilder were used (Finding 1), most of this would collapse into a
  shared builder chain.

  ---
  FINDING 6 (LOW) — Infrastructure change: CSkupiny seed in IntegrationTestFactoryBase

  The branch adds 5 CSkupiny records to the global seed in IntegrationTestFactoryBase.cs. The comment explains: "usp_zbv_tisk_excel
   tabulky 2–5 filtrují dle typ_investor" and "SkupinyExcelFinalDTO.DoplnHodnoty() volá .Single() pro skupiny s CisloSkupiny 1–4".

  This is correct — reference data (číselník) that all tests depend on belongs in the global seed. This follows the rule that base
  fixture provides shared reference data.

  ⚠️ Minor note: The existing base already had CSkupiny seeding for groups 1-5 (per agent research). If this is a duplicate, the
  second AddRange would cause a unique constraint violation at runtime. Verify this doesn't conflict with existing seed.

  ---
  SUMMARY TABLE

  ┌─────┬──────────┬──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │  #  │ Severity │                       Finding                        │                       Files                        │
  ├─────┼──────────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ 1   │ HIGH     │ No SeedBuilder usage — all DB writes are manual      │ All 8                                              │

  ├─────┼──────────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤

  What was changed yesterday

  Branch: develop_29036_ksk_PrintingBudget — uncommitted changes in 2 files.

  ---
  New endpoint added

  GET api/Reports/stavba/soupisZbv/data/web

  This is the /web variant of the existing stavba/soupisZbv/data endpoint.

  What it does differently:
  - Takes an extra Format query param (Pdf, Word, Excel, Dms, Prnx)
  - Iterates all ZBV on the given stavba, generates a report for each, merges all pages into one XtraReport, then exports via IOutputFormatter in the
  requested format
  - Returns the file directly (not a prnx blob for WPF)

  ---
  How to test it in Swagger / HTTP client

  Base URL: https://localhost:44301/api/Reports/stavba/soupisZbv/data/web

  Method: GET

  Query parameters:

  ┌──────────────────┬────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │      Param       │      Type      │                              Notes                              │
  ├──────────────────┼────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ StavbaId         │ int            │ ID of a stavba that has at least one ZBV                        │
  ├──────────────────┼────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ SPoznamkou       │ bool           │ true / false                                                    │
  ├──────────────────┼────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Format           │ enum           │ 0=Prnx, 1=Pdf, 2=Word, 3=Excel, 4=Dms                           │
  ├──────────────────┼────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ NastaveniTisku.* │ complex object │ all fields from TiskZbvNastaveniDTO sent as nested query params │
  └──────────────────┴────────────────┴─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  Minimal test call (PDF):
  GET /api/Reports/stavba/soupisZbv/data/web
      ?StavbaId=12345
      &SPoznamkou=false
      &Format=1
      &NastaveniTisku.SCenami=true
      &NastaveniTisku.Verze=1

  2. File downloads and opens correctly
  3. Test with a StavbaId that has no ZBV → expect 400 Bad Request with the localized message msgNaTetoStavbeNebylyNalezenyZadneZBV
  4. Multiple ZBV on same stavba → all pages merged into one file

 ---
  What was changed

  Only 2 files changed, both in TiskReportApi. The changes add a /web variant of an existing print endpoint.

  ---
  The existing desktop endpoint

  GET api/Reports/stavba/soupisZbv/data

  What it does:
  1. Finds all ZBV for the given StavbaId
  2. For each ZBV, generates a SoupisPraci report
  3. Merges all pages into one XtraReport
  4. Serializes it to a raw .prnx binary stream (DevExpress format)
  5. Returns application/octet-stream — a raw binary blob that only the WPF DevExpress viewer can open

  Who calls it from WPF:
  WidgetTiskStavba.xaml.cs:990
    → ucDXReportMain (DevExpress viewer)
      → SoupisPraciZbvStavbaPrint.VratData()
        → ReportServer.GetSoupisPraciZbvStavbaReport()
          → TiskApiClient.Reports_GetSoupisPraciZbvByStavbaAsync()   ← NSwag generated

  ---
  The new /web endpoint

  GET api/Reports/stavba/soupisZbv/data/web

  What is different:
  - Inherits all the same parameters (StavbaId, NastaveniTisku, SPoznamkou)
  - Adds one extra parameter: Format (enum: Pdf=1, Word=2, Excel=3, Dms=4, Prnx=0)
  - Instead of saving a .prnx stream, it resolves an IOutputFormatter keyed by Format and exports to the requested file type
  - Uses using XtraReport (properly disposes) — the old handler does not dispose it
  - Returns a real downloadable file (PDF, DOCX, XLSX) instead of a raw DevExpress blob

  Side-by-side:

  ┌─────────────────┬───────────────┬────────────────────────┐
  │                 │  Old (/data)  │    New (/data/web)     │
  ├─────────────────┼───────────────┼────────────────────────┤
  │ Extra param     │ —             │ Format enum            │
  ├─────────────────┼───────────────┼────────────────────────┤
  │ Output type     │ .prnx binary  │ PDF / Word / Excel     │
  ├─────────────────┼───────────────┼────────────────────────┤
  │ Dispose         │ ❌ no using   │ ✅ using XtraReport    │
  ├─────────────────┼───────────────┼────────────────────────┤
  │ Target consumer │ WPF DX viewer │ Web browser / React UI │
  └─────────────────┴───────────────┴────────────────────────┘

  ---
  How to call it from WPF UI

  Short answer: you don't — and you shouldn't.

  The /web endpoint is designed for the React web UI (Proconom_Web_App_TS). The WPF already has the original /data endpoint and uses it via the DevExpress
  print preview chain shown above.

  However, if you ever wanted to call it from WPF (e.g. to export a PDF directly without opening the DX viewer), the pattern would be:

  Step 1 — Regenerate NSwag client after committing the endpoint. NSwag will generate Reports_GetSoupisPraciZbvByStavbaWebAsync(...) in
  TiskApi/TiskApiClient.cs (same pattern as the existing Reports_GetKomplexniSoupisPraciDataWebAsync already in that file).

  Step 2 — Add a method to IReportServer / ReportServer.cs:
  // IReportServer.cs
  Task<FileResponse> GetSoupisPraciZbvStavbaReportWeb(
      int stavbaId, NastaveniTiskZBVDC nastaveni, bool sPoznamkou,
      OUTPUT_FORMAT2 format, CancellationToken cancellationToken);

  // ReportServer.cs
  public async Task<FileResponse> GetSoupisPraciZbvStavbaReportWeb(
      int stavbaId, NastaveniTiskZBVDC nastaveni, bool sPoznamkou,
      OUTPUT_FORMAT2 format, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
  {
      return await Execute(() =>
          ProconomStaticClient.AuthorizedTiskClient
              .Reports_GetSoupisPraciZbvByStavbaWebAsync(
                  stavbaId,
                  /* NastaveniTisku fields flattened one by one... */
                  nastaveni.Smernice, nastaveni.Uroven, ...,
                  sPoznamkou,
                  format,
                  cancellationToken),
          cancellationToken);
  }

  Step 3 — Use the returned FileResponse.Stream directly — save it to disk or open it via Process.Start, same as how DMS downloads work elsewhere in the
  app.

  ---
  But again — this endpoint is not meant for WPF. The web endpoint exists so the React UI can request a PDF/Word/Excel download directly from the browser
  without any DevExpress dependency.

  ---xxxxxxxxxxxxxxx

 ---
  All reused components — where they come from and what they do

  ---
  1. OUTPUT_FORMAT enum

  File: TiskReportApi/Enums/Enums.cs:127

  public enum OUTPUT_FORMAT
  {
      Prnx,   // 0 — DevExpress binary, WPF viewer only
      Pdf,    // 1 — PDF file
      Word,   // 2 — DOCX (not implemented yet)
      Excel,  // 3 — XLSX (not implemented yet)
      Dms     // 4 — save directly to DMS (not implemented yet)
  }

  This is the "what format do you want the file in?" selector passed by the caller. It's also used as the DI key when resolving the formatter — so Pdf →
  PdfFormatter, Prnx → PrnxFormatter, etc.

  ---
  2. IOutputFormatter interface

  File: TiskReportApi/Helpers/IOutputFormatter.cs

  public interface IOutputFormatter
  {
      Task<FileResponsePCM> ExportAsync(XtraReport report, string baseFileName);
  }

  Simple contract: takes a fully-built XtraReport + a base filename, returns a FileResponsePCM with the right stream and content-type. The caller doesn't
  care about the format — it just calls ExportAsync.

  ---
  3. Concrete formatters

  ┌────────────────┬──────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │     Class      │           File           │                 What it does                  │
  ├────────────────┼──────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ PdfFormatter   │ Helpers/PdfFormatter.cs  │ Calls report.ExportToPdf(ms) → returns .pdf   │
  ├────────────────┼──────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ PrnxFormatter  │ Helpers/PrnxFormatter.cs │ Calls report.SaveDocument(ms) → returns .prnx │
  ├────────────────┼──────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ WordFormatter  │ ❌ not yet created       │ commented out in Program.cs                   │
  ├────────────────┼──────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ ExcelFormatter │ ❌ not yet created       │ commented out in Program.cs                   │
  ├────────────────┼──────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ DmsFormatter   │ ❌ not yet created       │ commented out in Program.cs                   │
  └────────────────┴──────────────────────────┴───────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  Both existing formatters return ContentType = "application/octet-stream" — the browser triggers a file download either way. The difference is only the
  file extension and DevExpress export method used.

  ---
  4. DI registration — keyed services

  File: TiskReportApi/Program.cs:188

  builder.Services.AddKeyedSingleton<IOutputFormatter, PdfFormatter>(OUTPUT_FORMAT.Pdf);
  builder.Services.AddKeyedSingleton<IOutputFormatter, PrnxFormatter>(OUTPUT_FORMAT.Prnx);
  // Word, Excel, Dms — commented out, not yet implemented

  This uses .NET 8 keyed DI (AddKeyedSingleton). The key is the OUTPUT_FORMAT enum value. This is why the handler needs IKeyedServiceProvider (not plain
  IServiceProvider) to resolve by key:

  IOutputFormatter formatter = _serviceProvider
      .GetRequiredKeyedService<IOutputFormatter>(request.Format);

  Think of it like a dictionary: OUTPUT_FORMAT.Pdf → PdfFormatter. No switch statement needed.

  ---
  5. FileResponsePCM

  File: Commons/Response.cs:18

  public class FileResponsePCM
  {
      public Stream FileStream { get; set; }
      public string ContentType { get; set; }
      public string FileDownloadName { get; set; }
      public bool Inline { get; set; }   // true = open in browser, false = download
  }

  The shared return type used by all file-returning handlers in TiskReportApi. GeneralController.ResolveResponse checks if the MediatR result is
  FileResponsePCM and if so, builds the HTTP Content-Disposition header and returns a FileStreamResult.

  ---
  6. IKeyedServiceProvider vs IServiceProvider

  The first two /web handlers (GetDataProVymeruProvedenychPraciHandlerWeb, GetHromadnyZaznamMereniDataHandlerWeb) injected plain IServiceProvider. Your new
  handler uses IKeyedServiceProvider — this is the correct, explicit type. Both work because IServiceProvider in .NET 8 is backed by the same container that
   supports keyed lookups, but IKeyedServiceProvider makes the intent clearer.

  ---
  How the full pipeline fits together

  HTTP GET /stavba/soupisZbv/data/web?StavbaId=X&Format=1
          │
          ▼
  ReportsController.GetSoupisPraciZbvByStavbaWeb()
          │  [FromQuery] binds to GetSoupisPraciZbvByStavbaDataRequestWeb
          │
          ▼
  GeneralController.ResolveResponse()  ← calls _mediator.Send(request)
          │
          ▼
  GetSoupisPraciZbvByStavbaDataHandlerWeb.Handle()
          │
          ├── DB query: find all ZBV for StavbaId
          ├── foreach ZBV → _mediator.Send(GetSoupisPraciReportRequest)
          │      └── returns GetSoupisPraciReportResponse (data)
          ├── _reportService.GetReport(TYP_SESTAVA.SOUPIS_PRACI, data)
          │      └── returns XtraReport (built, not yet rendered)
          ├── report.ModifyDocument(x => x.AddPages(...))
          │      └── merges pages from each ZBV into one XtraReport
          │
          ├── IKeyedServiceProvider.GetRequiredKeyedService<IOutputFormatter>(Format)
          │      └── Format=1 (Pdf) → resolves PdfFormatter
          │
          └── formatter.ExportAsync(report, "SoupisPraciStavbyZbv")
                 └── PdfFormatter: report.ExportToPdf(ms) → FileResponsePCM
                          │
                          ▼
                GeneralController: sets Content-Disposition header
                          │
                          ▼
                HTTP 200  application/octet-stream  SoupisPraciStavbyZbv.pdf

  ---
  What's NOT yet implemented

  Word, Excel, Dms formatters are stubbed in the enum and commented out in Program.cs. If you call Format=2 (Word) right now, GetRequiredKeyedService will
  throw — no registration exists for that key.

  ---

https://tfs-server.proconom.local/ProconomSW/PROCONOM/_workitems/edit/29001/


in wpf ui in ucStavbaNastaveni.xaml there is dgSPravama that contains list of users that have access to that construction and dgBezPrav that just contains users with no rights to that construction. In StavbySectionControl.xaml is TreeView tvStavby if an user via context menu Treeview_Context_Opening chose to create a public constructions, then dgBezPrav is empty and dgSPravama contains all users cause is public one! That's the problem, now public constructions contains only 1 user in dgSPravama that user that just that constructions created, we need to have all users in. Summarize the task 


 FILE: EFModels/CustomModels/SStavby.cs
  METHOD: SStavby.Create()
  CHANGE: Removed else construction.InheritParentAccess(ctx) — for public constructions CUzivateleStavbyPristup now stays empty, so all users pass the deny-table filter in both TreeView
  APIs and GetStavbaUsersPermissionsHandler.

  INTENTIONALLY UNCHANGED — InheritParentAccess() method itself
  The method still exists in the file. It has an internal logic bug (! negation is inverted) but since it is now unreachable from Create() and there are no other callers, leaving it is
  safe. Deleting it would be a refactor beyond the scope of this fix.

❯ explain me SStavby.cs where last changes were done

● Read 1 file (ctrl+o to expand)

● This is a partial class — meaning this file adds extra methods on top of SStavby which is the EF entity generated from the database. Think of it like WPF code-behind: the generated part
  is the XAML-generated class, this file adds the business logic on top.

  ---
  The core concept — inverted access table

  CUzivateleStavbyPristup is a deny table, not an access table. It stores who is blocked:

  User NOT in table → has access ✓
  User IN table     → denied ✗

  This is the opposite of what you might expect. The method HasAccess (line 88) makes it clear:

  public bool HasAccess(int userId)
  {
      return !CUzivateleStavbyPristup.Any(x => x.KUzivatel == userId);
      // "I have access if I am NOT in the deny list"
  }

  WPF analogy: imagine a blacklist — if your name is on it, you're out. If the list is empty, everyone is allowed in.

  ---
  Create() — line 20

  The factory method. Called every time a construction is created. It:

  1. Checks permissions (lines 24–28) — can this user create a construction? Can they create a public one?
  2. Sets basic properties (lines 30–34) — code, name, parent utvar, creator
  3. Decides who gets blocked (lines 36–38) — this is the piece we changed
  4. Creates the "everyone" DMS group (line 41) — document permission group for all users

  ---
  The private vs public branch — the change we made

  Private construction (soukromaOsoba != null):

  construction.UpdateSoukromaOsoba(soukromaOsoba, ctx.CUzivatele.Where(x => x.XId != soukromaOsoba.XId));

  UpdateSoukromaOsoba (line 181) takes the owner and a list of everyone else, then adds every other user to CUzivateleStavbyPristup. Result: only the owner has access, everyone else is
  blocked.

  Public construction (before fix):

  construction.InheritParentAccess(ctx);   // ← was here, now removed

  InheritParentAccess (line 66) was supposed to inherit access restrictions from the parent utvar — but had the logic inverted (the ! on line 73 was backwards), so it ended up blocking
  most users instead of nobody. Both bugs came from here.

  Public construction (after fix):

  // public construction: CUzivateleStavbyPristup stays empty — all users have access

  Nothing is added to the deny table. Empty table = nobody blocked = everyone sees the construction in the TreeView and appears in dgSPravama.

  ---
  AddZakazanyPristup() — line 54

  Helper that adds one user to the deny table. Checks first if the user is already there to avoid duplicates. Called by UpdateSoukromaOsoba in a loop for private constructions.

  ---
  InheritParentAccess() — line 66

  Still exists in the file, now unreachable from Create(). Has an internal logic bug (misleading variable name + wrong ! negation on line 73 — the comment says "users who DON'T have parent
   utvar access" but the code selects users who DO have it). Left as-is since it is no longer called.

https://tfs-server.proconom.local/ProconomSW/PROCONOM/_workitems/edit/28949

složka připomínek se vytvoří v dané branch (jen pro testo) automaticky, najdu ji v DMS
stejně jako fungují stávající notifikace (definováno na API) pro přídání souboru do složky,
nastavit shodnou notifikaci pro jen přidání souboru do složky, pokusit se znovu použít i šablonu 
mailu

 The email notification chain after file upload:

  1. DSlozkaDC.cs:322 — the primary trigger point. After DokumentyUploadHelper.UploadMultiple() completes and the node is a SLOZKA (folder), it calls:
  await DokumentyServer.NotifyAfterBatch(dokumenty.Select(x => x.Kod).ToList(), this.Kod);
  2. DocumentManager.cs:102 — second trigger point, used when files are inserted via the document manager (e.g. drag & drop, multi-file operations):
  await server.NotifyAfterBatch(zDMS.Select(x => x.Kod).ToList(), folder.Kod);
  2. Note: only fires for DMS documents on a stavba (PrvekTyp == TYP_UZLU.STAVBA) and only if the folder is not ROOT.
  3. DokumentyServer.cs:253 — the actual API call:
  ProconomStaticClient.AuthorizedClient.Dokumenty_SendNotificationDocumentBatchAsync(...)
  3. This calls the backend (Test_Nove_API) which handles the actual email sending.

  There are also additional callers in WinExcelView.xaml.cs, DokumentyWidgetControl.xaml.cs, WFTaskDocumentsComponent.xaml.cs, AddReklamace.xaml.cs, AddToDo.xaml.cs, and winRestoreDocuments.xaml.cs — each calling NotifyAfterBatch after
   their specific upload scenarios.

  The main one for the "folder upload triggers email" scenario is DSlozkaDC.cs:322.

 Backend: Email notification after file upload

  Full chain

  POST documents/notification/batch/send
          ↓
  DokumentyController.SendNotificationDocumentBatch()     [DokumentyController.cs:636]
          ↓  MediatR ResolveResponse
  DocumentEmailUpdateHandler.Handle()                     [DocumentEmailUpdateHandler.cs:89]
          ↓
  DocumentEmailUpdateFactory.Make()                       [DocumentEmailUpdateFactory.cs:25]
          ↓
  EmailMessage.Send()  →  IEmailService.Send()            [NotificationMessage.cs:39]

  ---
  Step-by-step detail

  1. Handler (DocumentEmailUpdateHandler.cs:89)
  - Resolves the calling user and database from claims
  - Finds the stavba the folder belongs to
  - Loads the full folder path up to root (GetParentFolderPathIds)
  - Queries DSlozkyNotifikace — finds all users who subscribed to notifications on any folder in the path with AfterUpdate = true, Perioda = null (immediate, not periodic), and who are not the uploader
  - If nobody subscribed → returns early, no email sent
  - Builds DocumentEmailUpdateModel per recipient, separating deleted vs. non-deleted documents
  - Checks folder-level access: if a user lost access to the construction (CUzivateleStavbyPristup), their notification record is removed

  2. Factory (DocumentEmailUpdateFactory.cs:25)
  - Renders the email body via Razor view (IRazorViewFactory.GetEmailString)
  - Builds the subject via ISubjectFactory.GetSubject
  - Returns an EmailMessage instance

  3. EmailMessage.Check() gate (NotificationMessage.cs:55)
  Before sending, verifies:
  - User has NotifikaceMail == true in their settings
  - User has a valid email (BadMail override OR CUzivatele.Email)

  4. IEmailService.Send() (NotificationMessage.cs:43)
  The actual SMTP/provider send — implemented behind IEmailService.

  ---
  Key database table

  DSlozkyNotifikace — per-user, per-folder subscription record:
  - KUzivatel — who subscribed
  - KDokument — which folder
  - AfterUpdate — notify on upload
  - AfterDelete — notify on delete
  - Perioda — null = immediate; value = periodic digest

format datumu je
2024-01-31
/api/Reports/mereni/Stavba/{StavbaId}/Polozka/{PolozkaId}/Start/{Start}/Finish/{Finish}/DokumentPrvku/{DokumentPrvku}/DatumPodpisu/{DatumPodpisu}/Cerpani/{Cerpani}/OznaceniObjektu/{OznaceniObjektu}/TypSestavy/{TypSestavy}/web



What was the problem?

  The two report handlers — GetHromadnyZaznamMereniDataHandler (desktop = WPF, outputs .prnx) and GetHromadnyZaznamMereniDataHandlerWeb (web, outputs .pdf) — had
  near-identical logic (~120 lines each, copy-pasted). The only difference was the final export step (.SaveDocument() vs .ExportToPdf()). Classic duplication.

  ---
  What was done

  1. Enums.cs — new enum OUTPUT_FORMAT

  public enum OUTPUT_FORMAT { Prnx, Pdf, Word, Excel, Dms }
  Defines the supported output formats. Word, Excel, Dms are already planned but commented out in Program.cs.

  ---
  2. IOutputFormatter.cs — new interface (Strategy pattern)

  public interface IOutputFormatter
  {
      Task<FileResponsePCM> ExportAsync(XtraReport report, string baseFileName);
  }
  Abstracts the "how do I turn an XtraReport into a file response" concern. Each format is a separate implementation.

  ---
  3. PrnxFormatter.cs + PdfFormatter.cs — two new Strategy implementations

  - PrnxFormatter — calls report.SaveDocument(ms) → returns HromadnyZaznamMereni.prnx
  - PdfFormatter — calls report.ExportToPdf(ms) → returns HromadnyZaznamMereni.pdf

  Both return a FileResponsePCM with ContentType = "application/octet-stream".

  ---
  4. GetHromadnyZaznamMereniHandlerBase.cs — new abstract base class

  Extracted the shared logic from both handlers into two protected methods:

  - FetchReportDataAsync() — sends GetHromadnyZaznamMereniReportRequest via MediatR and returns the data
  - BuildCompositeReport() — constructs the final XtraReport (handles DokumentPrvku, RaditDokumentyPerProtokol, attachments, etc.)

  Both handlers now inherit from this base class — no more duplication.

  Two bugs fixed in the process:

  ┌─────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │         Bug         │                                   Original                                    │                        Fixed                         │
  ├─────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ LINQ result         │ hromadnyZaznamMereniReport.Prilohy.OrderByDescending(...) (no .ToList()) —    │ data.Prilohy =                                       │
  │ discarded           │ result thrown away                                                            │ data.Prilohy.OrderByDescending(...).ToList()         │
  ├─────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ MemoryStream not    │ MemoryStream ms = new() without using in desktop handler                      │ using MemoryStream ms = new() in base class          │
  │ disposed            │                                                                               │                                                      │
  └─────────────────────┴───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┴──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  ---
  5. GetHromadnyZaznamMereniDataHandler.cs — refactored (desktop handler)

  Before: 80+ lines of inline logic.
  After: 10 lines.

  // Constructor — injects PrnxFormatter by key
  [FromKeyedServices(OUTPUT_FORMAT.Prnx)] IOutputFormatter formatter

  // Handle
  GetHromadnyZaznamMereniResponse data = await FetchReportDataAsync(request, cancellationToken);
  using XtraReport composite = BuildCompositeReport(request, data);
  return await _formatter.ExportAsync(composite, "HromadnyZaznamMereni");

  Desktop is hardwired to Prnx — correct, WPF opens .prnx in the DevExpress report viewer.

  ---
  6. GetHromadnyZaznamMereniRequestWeb — Format property added

  public class GetHromadnyZaznamMereniRequestWeb : GetHromadnyZaznamMereniRequest
  {
      public OUTPUT_FORMAT Format { get; set; } = OUTPUT_FORMAT.Pdf;
  }

  Web callers can now choose the output format. Default = PDF.

  ---
  7. GetHromadnyZaznamMereniDataHandlerWeb — refactored (web handler)

  Before: 80+ lines of inline logic.
  After: 8 lines.

  // Constructor — injects IServiceProvider to resolve formatter at runtime
  public GetHromadnyZaznamMereniDataHandlerWeb(..., IServiceProvider sp)

  // Handle
  GetHromadnyZaznamMereniResponse data = await FetchReportDataAsync(request, cancellationToken);
  using XtraReport composite = BuildCompositeReport(request, data);
  IOutputFormatter formatter = _sp.GetRequiredKeyedService<IOutputFormatter>(request.Format);
  return await formatter.ExportAsync(composite, "HromadnyZaznamMereni");

  Web resolves the formatter dynamically at runtime based on request.Format — flexible for future Word/Excel/DMS support.

  ---
  8. Program.cs — DI registrations

  builder.Services.AddKeyedSingleton<IOutputFormatter, PdfFormatter>(OUTPUT_FORMAT.Pdf);
  builder.Services.AddKeyedSingleton<IOutputFormatter, PrnxFormatter>(OUTPUT_FORMAT.Prnx);
  // builder.Services.AddKeyedSingleton<IOutputFormatter, WordFormatter>(OUTPUT_FORMAT.Word);   // bude se implementovat
  // builder.Services.AddKeyedSingleton<IOutputFormatter, ExcelFormatter>(OUTPUT_FORMAT.Excel); // bude se implementovat
  // builder.Services.AddKeyedSingleton<IOutputFormatter, DmsFormatter>(OUTPUT_FORMAT.Dms);     // bude se implementovat

  Keyed singletons — each OUTPUT_FORMAT enum value maps to its formatter. Adding a new format in the future = add one line here + one new class.

  ---
  Summary

  ┌───────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────────┐
  │          Aspect           │                Before                │                 After                 │
  ├───────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Code duplication          │ ~120 lines copy-pasted in 2 handlers │ Shared base class                     │
  ├───────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Output format flexibility │ Hardcoded per handler                │ Runtime-selectable via request.Format │
  ├───────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Adding new format         │ Edit both handlers                   │ Add 1 class + 1 DI line               │
  ├───────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ LINQ bug                  │ Prilohy ordering silently discarded  │ Fixed                                 │
  ├───────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ MemoryStream leak         │ Not disposed in desktop handler      │ Fixed with using                      │
  └───────────────────────────┴──────────────────────────────────────┴───────────────────────────────────────┘
