https://tfs-server.proconom.local/ProconomSW/PROCONOM/_workitems/edit/28563/


● The key difference is already visible: the two grids bind to OsobyZP on different DataContext types:

  - ucStavbaNastaveni → DataClass.StavbyNastaveniDTO
  - ucObjektDetail → DataClass.ObjektDTO

  These are two different model classes — stavba-level vs. objekt-level. Let me confirm how each populates its OsobyZP.

● Searched for 1 pattern, read 2 files (ctrl+o to expand)

● Now let me find where ObjektDTO.OsobyZP is populated — what API endpoint and what data it pulls from.

● Searched for 2 patterns (ctrl+o to expand)

● Now I have enough to give you the full explanation.

  ---
  Root Cause: Different DataContext = Different Hierarchy Level

  Both grids bind to the same property name OsobyZP, but on completely different model objects:

  ┌─────────────┬───────────────────┬────────────────────┬────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │    Grid     │       File        │  DataContext type  │      Entity level      │               Source API               │
  ├─────────────┼───────────────────┼────────────────────┼────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ dgPodpisyZP │ ucStavbaNastaveni │ StavbyNastaveniDTO │ Stavba (site)          │ RozpocetDesktop_VratNastaveniStavby... │
  ├─────────────┼───────────────────┼────────────────────┼────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Podpisy     │ ucObjektDetail    │ ObjektDTO          │ Objekt (budget object) │ RozpocetDesktop_VratObjektAsync        │
  └─────────────┴───────────────────┴────────────────────┴────────────────────────┴────────────────────────────────────────┘

  They are reading different rows from the database — same PoziceVazby table, but filtered by TypUzlu and KodUzlu:

  - Stavba grid saves with KodUzlu = kodStavba (ucStavbaNastaveni.xaml.cs:2392)
  - Objekt grid saves with TypUzlu = TYP_UZLU.OBJEKT and KodUzlu = uloz.Kod (ucObjektDetail.xaml.cs:1173-1174)

  Why They Differ at Runtime

  The stavba-level OsobyZP are site-wide signatories set once in project settings. The objekt-level OsobyZP are per-object overrides — each ObjektDTO loads its own separate records from the API. Unless the backend explicitly
  copies the stavba records into each new objekt on creation, the objekt grid will be empty or have different values.

  There is a DefaultniPozice flag in the data (ucObjektDetail.xaml:1261 — IsReadOnly="{Binding DefaultniPoziceZP}"), which suggests "default positions" can be inherited/locked from the stavba level, but only if the backend
  populates them that way. The grid itself has no self-copying mechanism on the client side.

  In Short

  This is by design — they are not meant to be identical. The stavba grid shows site-level signatories; the objekt grid shows object-level signatories. If you expect them to match, the synchronization would have to happen on
  the server side (e.g., when creating/loading an objekt, the API should seed its OsobyZP from the stavba's OsobyZP). No such logic exists in the client code.




podpisy na nastavení stavby zdroj č.1 - použije se jako default
podpisy na SO zdroj č.2 (stavebním objektu) - použije se jako další jiný zdroj
podpisy na ZP zdroj č.2 (zjišťovacím protokolu) - převezme z nadřazené stavby

private void tbName_TextChanged(object sender, TextChangedEventArgs e)
{
    if (sender is not TextBox textBox || textBox.Text.Length < 200)
        return;

    textBox.TextChanged -= tbName_TextChanged;  // prevent re-entrancy

    MessageWindow.Dialog.Show(Properties.Resources.msgNazevNesmiMitPres200Znaku);
    textBox.Text = textBox.Text.Remove(textBox.Text.Length - 1);
    textBox.Select(textBox.Text.Length, 0);

    textBox.TextChanged += tbName_TextChanged;  // restore
}

https://tfs-server.proconom.local/ProconomSW/PROCONOM/_workitems/edit/28055/

prompt:
implement into private void ButtonGridExport_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e) in ToDoList.xaml.cs excel export,
data exported from dgToDos, button btnGridExport situate from left side next to existing btnPrintToDo

example excel
C:\Users\karel.skrabal\source\repos\Proconom\eConoM\WorkFlow\WorkFlowReporting.xaml.cs

private void ButtonGridExport_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
{
    if (listGridReports == null || listGridReports.Count == 0)
        return;

    Thread splash = SplasherHelper.ShowSplashWindow();
    //export excelu z gridu
    ExcelExportingOptions opts = new();
    opts.ExcelVersion = ExcelVersion.Xlsx;
    //vyhodit hidden sloupce
    List<string> hiddenColumns = dgGridReports.Columns.Where(x => x.IsHidden).Select(x => x.MappingName).ToList();
    opts.ExcludeColumns.AddRange(hiddenColumns);

    ExcelEngine exEngine = dgGridReports.ExportToExcel(dgGridReports.View, opts);
    IWorkbook workBook = exEngine.Excel.Workbooks[0];

    MemoryStream fileStream = new();
    workBook.SaveAs(fileStream);
    workBook.Close();
    exEngine.Dispose();

    // zobrazení          
    TiskApi.Aspose_SetupDC asposeSetup = new();
    asposeSetup.Kod_stavby = KodStavba;
    AsposeProconom.AsposeExcelPreview preview = new(KodStavba);
    preview.Title = Properties.Resources.strNahledDokumentu + " - " + Properties.Resources.strWorkflowReporting + " | xlsx";
    preview.nazev_souboru = "Workflow Reporty.xlsx";
    preview.Show();
    preview.fileApplied(fileStream, AsposeProconom.AsposeExcelPreview.LOCK_SHEET_TYPE.WF_GRID_REPORT, ProconomApi.ServerFaceSaveFormat.Xlsx, asposeSetup);

    splash.CloseSplasher();
}

https://tfs-server.proconom.local/ProconomSW/PROCONOM/_workitems/edit/27322/
--------------
rule of thumb
--------------

The diagnostic output reveals the real problem: The S key event never reaches your Window_KeyDown handler!
What's Happening:

✅ You press Ctrl → Event fires (e.Key: LeftCtrl)
❌ You press S → No event fired at Window level!
👉 The NumericTextBox (or RozpocetSectionSeznam) is consuming the event before it bubbles up

Solution: Use PreviewKeyDown Instead
WPF has two keyboard event types:

PreviewKeyDown → Tunnels DOWN (Window → Child) - fires first
KeyDown → Bubbles UP (Child → Window) - fires last

The issue is binding update timing. When you press Ctrl+S:

PreviewKeyDown fires BEFORE the textbox commits its value
The control still has focus and hasn't triggered LostFocus
The binding hasn't updated yet
You're saving the old value from the binding

When you click the button:
Button click causes textbox to lose focus
LostFocus triggers binding update
Then the save happens with the new value

prompt to keep the tread :

CONTEXT SNAPSHOT — DashboardTileType / Rozpočet / Měření
Problem

We need Rozpočet and Měření sections (ListViewItems) in a legacy WPF dashboard to behave exactly like btnOpenBIM:

correct visibility at startup

reacts to refresh

respects dashboard user settings

no DataContext dependency

no restart required

behavior preservation (no rewrite)

Existing architecture facts

App is legacy WPF, code-behind heavy

Dashboard visibility is controlled by:

DashboardTileType

DashBoardTileSetting

GetModuleRights(DashboardTileType)

ReloadTiles(...)

btnOpenBIM uses dashboard tile logic, NOT maPravoCist, NOT DataContext

maPravoCist(...) is section-level security and must stay in click handlers only

Refresh (btnRefresh) already updates dashboard tiles correctly

Critical constraint

DashboardTileType currently lives in ProconomApiClient.cs
→ this file is NSwag-generated
→ MUST NOT be edited (changes would be overwritten)

Key conclusion (agreed)

Rozpočet & Měření must behave like dashboard tiles

Therefore they must NOT depend on DataContext or Stavba

Visibility must come from the same source of truth as btnOpenBIM

Generated API enums must not be extended

Architectural decision

DO NOT modify ProconomApiClient.cs or any NSwag-generated enum.

Instead:

Introduce or use a UI-owned dashboard enum (e.g. DashboardTileTypeUi)

OR introduce a mapping layer between API tile types and UI features

Reuse existing dashboard pipeline:

settings → tiles → GetModuleRights(...) → refresh

Bind ListView visibility to this dashboard logic

Keep maPravoCist(...) only in MouseDown / action logic

Explicit non-goals

No MVVM rewrite

No Dispatcher hacks

No DataContext-based visibility

No editing generated code

No duplication of permission logic

Open design task (next step)

Decide how to extend dashboard logic without touching NSwag code, e.g.:

UI-level enum wrapping API enum

Dictionary mapping API DashboardTileType → UI features

Adapter/helper around GetModuleRights

-----

https://tfs-server.proconom.local/ProconomSW/PROCONOM/_workitems/edit/27536/

a classic WPF nested ScrollViewer + MouseWheel routing conflict

Correct design rule (key insight) :
Inner ScrollViewer should scroll first
Outer ListView should scroll only if inner cannot

The error is caused by this line being executed when the visual tree is NOT ready yet:

this.AddHandler(
    UIElement.MouseWheelEvent,
    new MouseWheelEventHandler(OnGlobalMouseWheel),
    handledEventsToo: true);


In WPF, calling AddHandler in a Window constructor before the visual tree is fully created can throw a NullReferenceException during build/XAML compilation, which Visual Studio reports as:

Unknown build error

This is a known WPF/XAML quirk, especially with:

code-behind event wiring

third-party controls

compiled XAML (.g.cs)

SectionControl.xaml.cs .... App.Login.PravaModuly.IsBOZPAllowed

Due to operator precedence (&& binds tighter than ||), this is evaluated as:
if ((App.Login.PravaModuly.IsBOZPAllowed && MaPravoCist(TYP_MODUL.BOZP)) || true)
Result: The condition is ALWAYS TRUE because of || true

namespace proconom.Admin.GroupManager
public class GroupManagerContext : BaseObservableObject
        private void UpdatePermisionEntities() { PermissionEntities = GetPermisionEntities(UseUsers ? FetchUser : FetchGroup); }
        private TrueObservableCollection<BasePermissionEntity> GetPermisionEntities(Func<BasePermissionEntity, bool> filter)
        {
            if (OriginalPermissionEntities is null)
                return null;
            if (!OriginalPermissionEntities.Any())
                return new();
            return new(OriginalPermissionEntities.Where(filter));
        }


v těchto 2 metodách se filtruje buď na přetypování a když tak pak na propertu Name,SurName (pro User)
nebo na Name (DocumentGroup) musí se přidat ShowableName properta pro objekt typu DocumentGroup!!!!
